Motivation: 仿真训练的操作策略往往依赖特权状态信息才能学到有效的接触丰富行为，但实际部署只能使用部分观测（如含噪相机图像）。常用的 teacher-student 蒸馏需要学生同时推断任务相关状态并重新学习专家已有的动作映射，效率低且未必最优；而另一种仅学习感知接口、最小化状态估计误差的方法，并不一定能最小化该估计带来的下游控制误差。

Method: 训练一个视觉状态估计器，损失包含两部分：对状态估计的直接监督，以及通过冻结的可微专家反向传播的动作一致性损失。采用调度式目标：先建立物理上有意义的状态估计，再逐步提高对影响专家动作的误差的权重。专家本身保持冻结不重新训练。

Result: 在五个目标条件操作任务中，保留专家（仅训练感知接口）的做法始终优于从同一专家演示语料直接做像素到动作的模仿学习。在真实 Panda 机器人上实现了 sim-to-real 迁移，无需重新训练底层专家即达到 76% 的成功率。

Conclusion: 与其让学生策略重新学习专家已掌握的动作映射，不如冻结专家、仅学习一个能补偿缺失状态输入的感知接口；且用动作一致性损失（而非单纯最小化状态误差）来训练该接口，能更有效地减少下游控制误差，并具备良好的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Simulation-trained manipulation policies can exploit privileged state information to learn effective contact-rich behaviours, but deployment requires acting from partial observations such as noisy camera images. A common solution is teacher-student distillation, in which a visuomotor policy is trained to reproduce the actions of the privileged expert. This requires the student to jointly infer the task-relevant state and relearn the expert's action mapping that is already available. An alternative is to reuse the state-based expert and learn only a perceptual interface that reconstructs its missing state inputs. However, minimising the state estimate error alone does not necessarily minimise the downstream control error induced by these estimates. To bridge this gap, we train a visual state estimator using both direct state supervision and an action-consistency loss backpropagated through the frozen, differentiable expert. A scheduled objective first establishes a physically meaningful state estimate and progressively emphasises errors that affect the expert's actions. Across five goal-conditioned manipulation tasks, retaining the expert consistently outperforms direct pixel-to-action imitation from the same expert demonstration corpus. We further demonstrate sim-to-real transfer on a physical Panda robot, achieving 76% success without retraining the underlying expert.

</details>


### [109] [Quantile Head for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34061)
*Xuan Wang,Yinan Wu,Haoran Duan,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Quantile Head，将回归与流匹配统一到同一目标并扩展出分位数目标，一次前向即可输出有序的边际动作分位数，支持多种采样策略且联合监督训练中位数策略，在多个基准和真实机器人任务上取得最高成功率与最短耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型的动作头存在明显局限：点回归只能给出动作分布的点估计，而标准流匹配采样器需要代价高昂的迭代采样。作者希望解决这两类方法的不足。

Method: 将回归与流匹配统一在一个共享目标下，并扩展推导出分位数目标。基于该目标设计 Quantile Head：一次前向预测中位数与正值间隔，构成有序的边际动作分位数；这些分位数无需重训练即可支持多种采样策略，并通过联合监督训练默认的中位数策略。作者还对联合监督进行了局部分析。

Result: 局部分析表明，在邻近分位数经过校准、间隔固定且校正速度匹配的情况下，直接更新中位数比仅用中位数监督具有更低的方差。实验上，联合监督的中位数策略在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、LIBERO-Pro 以及两个真实机器人任务上取得对比方法中最高的平均成功率，并在匹配的 LIBERO 基线中具有最短的平均回合时间。

Conclusion: 通过统一回归与流匹配并引入分位数目标，Quantile Head 兼顾了点估计的效率与分布建模的能力，实现一次前向的多样采样与更低方差的中位数训练，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models integrate pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with action heads for robot control. Common action heads have distinct limitations: point regression provides only a point estimate of the action distribution, while standard flow-matching samplers require costly iterative sampling. To address these limitations, we unify regression and flow matching under a shared objective and extend it to derive a quantile objective. This quantile objective guides the design of our Quantile Head, which predicts a median and positive gaps to form ordered marginal action quantiles in one forward pass. These quantiles support multiple sampling strategies without retraining and are jointly supervised to train the default median policy. Our local analysis of this joint supervision shows that, with calibrated nearby quantiles, fixed gaps, and matched correction speed, direct median updates have lower variance than under median-only supervision. Experiments show that this jointly supervised median policy achieves the highest average success rates among the compared methods on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, LIBERO-Pro, and two real-robot tasks, together with the shortest mean episode time among matched LIBERO baselines; code is available at https://github.com/xwangrs/Quantile-Head-for-VLA.

</details>


### [110] [AD-E2E-JEPA: A Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture For End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34085)
*Haoran Zhu,Wancong Zhang,Yann LeCun,Anna Choromanska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估现有JEPA世界模型在端到端自动驾驶中的表现，并提出AD-E2E-JEPA，通过SIGReg正则化投影器大幅压缩patch数量和嵌入维度，实现100倍推理加速并保持规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要能理解物理世界、推理规划并安全运行的世界模型。现有JEPA世界模型在端到端自动驾驶中存在两难：要么对驾驶准确但计算昂贵，要么计算高效但不足以支撑规划。作者希望解决这一权衡问题。

Method: 首先在目标条件零样本规划设定下（使用真实未来观测作为目标、不训练任何驾驶策略）系统评估LeWM、DINO-WM、JEPA-WM等动作条件JEPA世界模型。然后提出AD-E2E-JEPA，引入SIGReg正则化的可学习投影器作用于投影后的patch嵌入，将规划patch数量减少16倍、嵌入维度减少4倍。

Result: 实现100倍推理加速并保持规划性能，256条候选轨迹上8帧rollout运行时间0.8秒。不训练驾驶策略时，在256/8192候选轨迹词汇下世界模型rollout平均20米目标位移分别达4.0/2.8米。NAVSIMv2基准上，目标条件零样本规划达67.3/72.9 EPDMS（含乘法安全指标）和84.1/86.5 EPDMS†（不含）。自监督预训练投影器将下游模仿学习从80.2提升至85.4 EPDMS。

Conclusion: AD-E2E-JEPA通过SIGReg正则化投影器有效解决了JEPA世界模型在自动驾驶中精度与效率的权衡，在保持规划性能的同时大幅提升推理速度，且自监督预训练投影器能提升下游模仿学习性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires \textit{world models} that can understand the physical world, reason and plan, and operate safely. In this paper, we first systematically evaluate existing action-conditioned joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) world models, including LeWM, DINO-WM, and JEPA-WM for end-to-end autonomous driving (E2EAD). To isolate world-model quality from policy learning, we employ a goal-conditioned zero-shot planning setting that evaluates these models using ground-truth future observations as goals, without training any driving policy. We find that existing JEPA-based world models are either accurate for driving but computationally expensive, or computationally efficient but insufficient for planning. To address this trade-off, we propose \textbf{AD-E2E-JEPA}, which introduces a SIGReg-regularized learnable projector applied to projected patch embeddings. The projector reduces the number of planning patches by $16\times$ and the embedding dimension by $4\times$, achieving a $100\times$ inference speedup while retaining planning performance, with a 0.8-second runtime for an 8-frame rollout over 256 candidate trajectories. \textit{Without} training any driving policy, the world model itself reaches the goals located 20 meters away on average within the displacement of respectively 4.0/2.8 meters, using world-model rollouts over trajectory vocabularies of respectively 256/8,192 candidates. On the NAVSIMv2 benchmark, it achieves 67.3/72.9 EPDMS with multiplicative safety metrics and 84.1/86.5 EPDMS$^{\dagger}$ without them in goal-conditioned zero-shot planning. Experiments further show that the self-supervised pretrained projector improves downstream imitation learning performance from 80.2 to 85.4 EPDMS. The source code is available at https://github.com/HaoranZhuExplorer/AD-E2E-JEPA

</details>


### [111] [Beyond Retrieval Relevance: Scene-Grounded Risk Entailment for Vision-Language Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34145)
*Jiaxin Liu,Ruilin Yu,Liang Peng,Jingkai Wang,Chengxiang Zhao,Zhenxin Zhu,Bing Wang,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Hong Wang,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出在VLM决策前引入驾驶风险知识图谱(DRKG)与SWRL推理阶段，将检索到的风险知识转化为场景适用的结构化证据，从而弥合相关性—适用性差距，并在nuReasoning基准上提升了规划安全指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG为视觉-语言驾驶系统提供外部安全知识，但检索到的风险规则可能仅语义相关却不适用于当前场景。VLM需自行完成物体落地、跨时间实体绑定与关系验证，导致风险结论的支撑依据是隐式的、不可靠的，存在"相关性—适用性"鸿沟。

Method: 构建驾驶风险知识图谱(DRKG)，并采用语义网规则语言(SWRL)进行推理：结构化感知结果实例化为场景事实，SWRL规则在前件共同满足时推导事件与有向风险关系；将识别出的事件、绑定后的风险关系及激活规则的语义描述组成紧凑证据，用于条件化VLM与扩散规划器。

Result: 在nuReasoning上进行匹配对比实验，相比基于相关性检索的基线，NPS提升1.30分，非自身过失碰撞得分(NC)提升2.76分。

Conclusion: 结果表明，场景适用的风险证据相比语义检索式的风险知识更能改善安全加权规划，验证了显式、可验证的风险推理对驾驶决策的价值。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) gives vision--language driving systems access to external safety knowledge, yet a retrieved risk rule may be relevant without applying to the current scene. A vision--language model (VLM) receiving such knowledge must ground objects, bind entities across time, and verify relations before deciding how to act, leaving the support for risk conclusions implicit. We address this relevance--applicability gap with a Driving-Risk Knowledge Graph (DRKG) and Semantic Web Rule Language (SWRL) reasoning stage before VLM decision-making. Structured perception instantiates scene facts, from which SWRL rules derive events and directed risk relations when their antecedents are jointly satisfied. Recognized events, bound risk relations, and semantic descriptions of activated rules form compact evidence that conditions the VLM and diffusion planner. In matched comparisons on nuReasoning, our method improved the nuReasoning planning score (NPS) by 1.30 points and the non-at-fault collision score (NC) by 2.76 points over the relevance retrieval-based baseline. These gains indicate that scene-applicable risk evidence improves safety-weighted planning relative to semantically retrieved risk knowledge.

</details>


### [112] [Reliability-Aware Sparse Route Memory for Round-Trip Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34163)
*Bojun Long,Lingfan Bao,Tianhu Peng,Jingcheng Sun,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究连续往返式视觉语言导航（Round-trip VLN），提出一种可靠性感知的稀疏路径记忆机制，将去程轨迹记录为有序几何锚点，并通过结构化提示、动作级仲裁和终止验证反向查询，从而提升机器人返回起点的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN通常被当作单向任务评估，但实际部署的机器人到达目标后往往需要返回。作者诊断出往返导航中的三类失败：方向可观测性、偏差恢复与终止稳定性。

Method: 提出可靠性感知的稀疏路径记忆（reliability-aware sparse route memory），把已执行的去程轨迹记录为有序几何锚点；返回时通过结构化提示（structured hint）、动作级仲裁（action-level arbitration）和终止验证（terminal verification）反向查询这些锚点。在NaVILA与仿真Unitree Go2上，用50个反向配对回合进行评估，并与仅语言返回及拥有精确路径信息的基线对比。

Result: 仅语言返回成功率22.0%；在线系统达55.1%；在拥有精确路径信息时，相同接口可达86.0%。剩余在线差距主要来自几何证据不可靠而无法授权干预。

Conclusion: 有效的返回需要准确信息与基于该信息的一致动作同时具备；信息质量、行为一致性与在线可靠性构成长期导航中相互独立的限制因素。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) is typically evaluated as a one-way task, although deployed robots may need to return after reaching a goal. We study continuous round-trip VLN and diagnose failures in directional observability, deviation recovery, and termination stability. We propose a reliability-aware sparse route memory that records the executed Outbound trajectory as ordered geometric anchors and queries them in reverse through a structured hint, action-level arbitration, and terminal verification. On 50 reverse-paired episodes using NaVILA and a simulated Unitree Go2, language-only Return succeeds in 22.0% of episodes, while our online system reaches 55.1%. With exact route information, the same interfaces achieve 86.0%, showing that effective Return requires both accurate information and consistent action on that information. The remaining online gap arises mainly from geometric evidence that is too unreliable to authorise intervention. These results distinguish information quality, behavioural consistency, and online reliability as separate limits in long-horizon navigation.

</details>


### [113] [RAVEL: Asynchronous Rolling Inference for Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34170)
*Yuhan Chen,Ke Yu,Pengfei Liu,Shuxun Wang,Yi Yang,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAVEL 是一个异步推理框架，通过滚动缓冲区的部分去噪动作复用和 VLM 编码与动作生成的解耦，显著降低流式 VLA 模型的推理延迟，同时保持原有任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式（flow-based）视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在通用机器人操作中表现优异，但依赖计算昂贵的 VLM 编码和多步迭代动作生成，造成严重的延迟瓶颈，使机器人在动态环境中难以快速响应。本文旨在解决这一推理延迟问题。

Method: 提出 RAVEL（Rolling Asynchronous VLA Enabling Low-Latency Control）异步推理框架，针对 VLM 主干与动作专家两处瓶颈分别优化：1）采用滚动缓冲区将部分去噪的未来动作向前传递，使近期动作只需一次去噪步骤即可执行；2）将 VLM 编码与滚动动作生成解耦，动作专家持续使用最新的 VLM 上下文运行，并通过轻量级快速观测通路（FOP）直接以当前观测为条件。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务中，RAVEL 一致地实现显著更低的响应延迟，同时保持底层 VLA 的任务能力，支持高频、响应式的闭环控制。

Conclusion: RAVEL 通过异步解耦与滚动缓冲机制有效缓解 VLA 推理延迟瓶颈，在维持任务性能的前提下实现低延迟、高频的机器人闭环控制。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models are highly effective for generalist robot manipulation, yet their reliance on computationally expensive VLM encoding and multi-step iterative action generation imposes a significant latency bottleneck. The resulting inference latency makes it difficult for robots to respond quickly, especially in dynamic environments. We address this limitation with RAVEL (Rolling Asynchronous VLA Enabling Low-Latency Control), an asynchronous inference framework that addresses the computational bottlenecks of both the VLM backbone and the action expert. To reduce the delay from multi-step action denoising, RAVEL allows near-term actions to be executed after a single denoising step by carrying partially denoised future actions forward in a rolling buffer. To avoid blocking on slow VLM encoding, RAVEL decouples VLM encoding from rolling action generation, allowing the action expert to operate continuously using the latest available VLM context, while a lightweight Fast Observation Pathway (FOP) directly conditions the action expert on current observations. Across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, RAVEL consistently achieves substantially lower response latency while maintaining the task capability of the underlying VLA, enabling high-frequency and responsive closed-loop control.

</details>


### [114] [FailPatch: Failure Residual Patching for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34175)
*Peng Yu,Jiacheng Wang,Ziheng Zhang,Xuchong Zhang,Baoting Li,Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuxiang Chen,Tiancai Wang,Hongbin Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FailPatch：一种失败驱动的残差补丁框架，在冻结的VLA策略的动作隐空间中引入Null门控残差专家库，用0.52%可训练参数将长时程任务的部署失败状态重定向至成功区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略通常依赖成功示范进行适应：示范提供直接的动作监督，却几乎不覆盖易失败状态；而部署中暴露的失败状态又缺乏可用于监督学习的纠正动作。因此需要将"动作监督"与"执行可靠性监督"解耦。

Method: 利用"成功示范规定如何行动、部署轨迹指示何时行为不可靠"的分工；观察到动作隐表征在可靠状态与失败关联状态之间具有清晰线性可分性，且直接条件化动作生成。FailPatch在冻结VLA策略的动作隐空间中插入Null门控残差专家库（Null-gated Residual Expert Bank），并用统一的Preserve–Redirect–Trust目标实现：在可靠状态保留原策略、在失败关联状态选择残差专家、并在有界干预下将隐表征从失败区域重定向到成功关联区域。

Result: 仅需0.52%可训练参数。在四个长时程RoboTwin任务上：干净评估成功率提升11.0个百分点，干净到随机的泛化场景提升9.5个百分点；在三个真实世界任务上较基线提升16.7个百分点。

Conclusion: FailPatch表明，将部署失败作为可靠性监督信号、并在动作隐空间做有界残差重定向，可在极小参数开销下显著提升VLA策略的部署成功率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are typically adapted using successful demonstrations, which provide direct action supervision but rarely cover failure-prone states. Deployment failures expose these states, yet lack the corrective actions needed for conventional supervised learning. We propose FailPatch, a failure-driven residual patching framework that decouples action supervision from execution-reliability supervision. Successful demonstrations ground how the policy should act, while deployment trajectories indicate when its behavior becomes unreliable. We further observe that action hidden representations exhibit clear linear separability between reliable and failure-associated states while directly conditioning action generation. Building on these insights, FailPatch introduces a Null-gated Residual Expert Bank into the action hidden space of a frozen VLA policy. A unified Preserve--Redirect--Trust objective retains the original policy in reliable states, selects residual experts in failure-associated states and redirects representations from failure regions toward success-associated regions under bounded intervention. With only 0.52% trainable parameters, FailPatch improves success rates by 11.0 percentage points on four long-horizon RoboTwin tasks under clean evaluation, 9.5 percentage points under clean-to-random generalization, and 16.7 percentage points over the baseline across three real-world tasks. Project and code: https://github.com/yupeng-2003/FailPatch.

</details>


### [115] [Unified Visual-Tactile-Action Modeling from Human Demonstrations for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34182)
*Wenqiao Li,Qianyou Zhao,Jiawen Hao,Xuezhou Zhu,Tengyu Liu,Kaifeng Zhang,Chuan Wen,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文利用人类触觉数据提升机器人灵巧操作策略，构建了同步采集视觉、触觉与手部动作的触觉动作捕捉系统及UVTA数据集，并提出统一视觉-触觉-动作模型，将人类与机器人本体映射到对齐的触觉与动作表征中，联合预测未来动作与触觉轨迹，在真实机器人五任务上取得70%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需要触觉反馈，但机器人触觉演示难以规模化，因为灵巧手遥操作给操作者的触觉反馈有限；相比之下，人类演示能提供更可扩展且多样的触觉交互数据。论文基于一个简单前提：手可以改变，但交互背后的物理规律不变，因此希望借助人类触觉数据改进灵巧操作策略。

Method: 首先构建触觉动作捕捉系统，同步记录图像、触觉信号与手部动作，并据此构建UVTA数据集，涵盖5个接触密集型任务，包含1,000个人类演示（覆盖多样交互模式）以及每任务150个机器人演示。随后提出统一视觉-触觉-动作模型（Unified Visual-Tactile-Action Model），将人类与机器人两种本体映射为对齐的触觉与动作表征，并联合预测未来动作与触觉轨迹；该联合目标使人类演示可监督接触感知表征学习，而部署时仅执行机器人动作。

Result: 在5个真实机器人任务评估中，该方法平均成功率达70%，显著优于最强视觉-触觉基线（29%）以及架构消融版本（42%）。性能随人类演示数量增加而持续提升，在每任务1,000个演示时仍未饱和，验证了可扩展人类触觉数据对灵巧操作的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文证明人类触觉演示是可扩展的有效数据来源，通过将人类与机器人交互映射到对齐的触觉-动作表征并联合预测动作与触觉轨迹，能够显著提升真实机器人灵巧操作性能，且性能随人类数据规模增长而持续改善、未见饱和。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires tactile feedback.However, robot tactile demonstrations are difficult to scale,because dexterous-hand teleoperation provides limited tactile feedback to the operator. In contrast, human demonstrations offer a substantially more scalable source of diverse tactile interactions. Motivated by a simple premise: hands can change, but the underlying physics of interaction does not. We leverage human tactile data to improve dexterous manipulation policies. Specifically, we first build a tactile motion-capture system that synchronously records images, tactile signals, and hand motions. Using this system, we construct the UVTA dataset spanning five contact-rich tasks, with 1,000 human demonstrations covering diverse interaction patterns and 150 robot demonstrations per task. To transfer the underlying physics of human interaction to robot control, we propose a Unified Visual-Tactile-Action Model that maps both embodiments into aligned tactile and action representations and jointly predicts future action and tactile trajectories. The joint objective enables human demonstrations to supervise contact-aware representation learning, while only robot actions are executed during deployment. In real-robot evaluations across five tasks, our method achieves an average success rate of 70%, outperforming the strongest visual-tactile baseline, which achieves 29%, and an architecture ablation, which achieves 42%. Performance improves consistently with additional human demonstrations and exhibits no saturation at 1,000 demonstrations per task, validating the effectiveness of scalable human tactile data for dexterous manipulation. Project page is available at https://uni-vta.github.io/.

</details>


### [116] [WB-WAM: Heterogeneous Body-Hand Pre-training for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34199)
*Chuan Qin,Shaoting Zhu,Siyuan Luo,Siqiao Huang,Hongyu Zhao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WB-WAM世界动作模型，将显式全身动作监督引入生成式视频预训练，实现数据高效的类人机器人移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人的移动操作需要身体与手的协调行为，而传统机器人预训练数据对此类全身运动的覆盖十分有限，导致数据效率低。

Method: 构建共享物理动作空间，整合来自异构数据源的躯干、根部和灵巧手标注，从1880.2小时部分标注视频与运动数据中进行联合视频与动作学习；所得先验经PICO中期训练精炼，并通过辅助正向运动学监督适配到机器人任务；构建WB-Datasets，包含重定向的第一人称人类演示与机器人轨迹。

Result: 仿真中HumanoidArena达81.9%；真实世界五个任务平均成功率84.0%；任务对齐的PICO中期训练提升下游任务表现并减少真机演示需求。

Conclusion: 异构全身预训练与人类运动迁移是通往数据高效类人移动操作的可行路径。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation demands coordinated body and hand behavior, while conventional robot pre-training data provide limited coverage of such whole-body motion. We present WB-WAM, a World Action Model that incorporates explicit whole-body action supervision into generative video pre-training. A shared physical action space integrates body, root, and dexterous hand annotations from heterogeneous sources, enabling joint video and action learning from 1880.2 hours of partially annotated video and motion data. The resulting priors are refined through PICO mid-training and adapted to robot tasks with auxiliary forward kinematics supervision. We construct WB-Datasets to support these stages with retargeted egocentric human demonstrations and robot trajectories, allowing task-aligned human motion to supplement limited robot data. Evaluations in simulation demonstrate strong whole-body task performance with 81.9% in HumanoidArena, while real-world experiments further validate WB-WAM with 84.0% mean success across five tasks. Moreover, task-aligned PICO mid-training improves downstream task performance while reducing the need for real-robot demonstrations. These results support heterogeneous whole-body pre-training and human motion transfer as a practical route to data-efficient humanoid loco-manipulation.

</details>


### [117] [RLE-Bench: A Qualifying Exam for Coding Agents as Robot Learning Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34210)
*Haitong Ma,Chenxiao Gao,Rushi Qiang,Na Li,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RLE-Bench，一个面向机器人学习任务的基准测试，覆盖交互控制、策略学习、感知估计与机械设计四类工作流，用以评估编码智能体的工程能力，并汇总为 RLE Index。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准测试多聚焦单个产物的性能（如策略或控制器），覆盖编码智能体更广泛的工程能力有限。真实机器人场景还要求智能体在资源约束下构建、集成、诊断和改进异构产物，并从多模态反馈中推理。

Method: 构建 RLE-Bench 基准，包含四类代表性机器人开发工作流任务：交互控制、策略学习、感知与估计、机械设计。针对智能体提交的产物采用多样的任务特定指标进行评估，涵盖智能体实现的成功率、训练出的策略智能体、构建的测试框架以及设计的机械结构，并汇总为整体 RLE Index，同时报告各工作流的能力画像。

Result: 通过 RLE Index 与工作流特定能力画像，实现对编码智能体在多个能力维度上的系统比较；并提供排名之外的深入案例研究。

Conclusion: 案例研究揭示智能体在代表性任务上的行为，指出当前能力与局限，并说明机器人任务为未来智能体训练提供的机遇。

Abstract: Coding agents are beginning to move beyond purely digital tasks to tackle physical-world challenges, particularly in robotics. Existing robotics benchmarks, however, primarily focus on the performance of individual artifacts, such as policies or controllers, offering limited coverage of coding agents' broader engineering capabilities. Real-world robotics extends beyond control: agents must build, integrate, diagnose, and improve heterogeneous artifacts under resource constraints and reason from multimodal feedback. To evaluate these broader capabilities, we introduce RLE-Bench, a benchmark of robot-learning tasks spanning four representative robotics development workflows: interactive control, policy learning, perception and estimation, and mechanical design. We use diverse task-specific metrics to evaluate the artifacts submitted by the coding agents, from the success rate the agents achieved to the policy agents trained, the harness agent built, and the mechanical structures the agent designed. We aggregate these metrics into an overall RLE Index and report workflow-specific capability profiles, enabling systematic comparison of coding agents' capabilities across multiple capability dimensions. Beyond performance ranks, we also conduct in-depth case studies examining agent behavior on representative tasks, highlighting both current capabilities and limitations, and pointing to the opportunities robotics tasks have to offer for future agent training.

</details>


### [118] [mmHRI: Towards Privacy-Preserving Human-Robot Interaction with Millimeter-Wave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34220)
*Junqiao Fan,Yuxuan Hu,Bofan Lyu,Yanshuo Lu,Pengfei Liu,Jiarui Zhang,Fangqiang Ding,Lihua Xie,Gen Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出mmHRI，首个利用毫米波雷达实现隐私保护人机交互的多模态机器人操作框架，在隐私场景下达到85.09%动作识别准确率并完成闭环递送任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRI系统多依赖RGB相机持续观测人类以响应手势等非语言指令，但在医院病房、餐厅等隐私敏感环境中，直接摄像观测人类受到限制，因此需要一种不依赖可识别图像的隐私保护型HRI方案。

Method: 利用毫米波雷达穿透隐私屏障感知人体运动；针对雷达数据稀疏与时间不一致问题，设计双流架构（同时从原始雷达张量与雷达点云学习，估计人体动作与3D姿态），并引入基于记忆的状态空间模型（MSSM）保留历史雷达特征以抑制姿态/动作突变；再将估计的人体状态转为结构化文本指令，驱动VLA策略实现闭环机器人操作与人类感知反应。

Result: 在隐私保护帘幕场景下，动作识别准确率达85.09%，优于现有基于雷达的方案；机器人试验在视觉遮挡下成功完成递送与取回，并在未见受试者、杂乱配置及环境中保持稳定性能。

Conclusion: mmHRI验证了毫米波雷达引导的隐私保护HRI在真实机器人操作中的可行性，能够在视觉受阻条件下实现稳定的人体动作/姿态估计与闭环操作，为隐私敏感环境的辅助机器人交互提供了新思路。

Abstract: Assistive robots increasingly operate in many human-centered environments and perform various human-robot interaction (HRI) tasks, such as object delivery. However, most existing HRI systems rely on RGB cameras that continuously observe humans to respond to non-verbal commands, such as hand gestures. This raises privacy concerns in privacy- critical environments, such as hospital wards or restaurants, where direct camera observation of humans is restricted. To develop privacy-preserving HRI, we leverage millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar, which can sense human motion through privacy barriers without identifiable imagery. We propose mmHRI, the first multi-modal robot manipulation framework that achieves mmWave radar-guided privacy-preserving HRI. mmHRI introduces two key designs to mitigate the sparsity and temporal inconsistency of radar data in cluttered robot manipulation environments. First, we propose a dual-stream architecture that jointly learns from unfiltered raw radar tensors and radar point clouds to estimate both human actions and 3D poses. To mitigate signal inconsistency, mmHRI further incorporates a memory-based state-space model (MSSM) that retains historical radar features to reduce abrupt changes in pose/action. These estimated human states are then converted into structured textual robot instructions, which control a vision-language-action (VLA) policy for closed-loop robot manipulation and human-aware reactions. Our evaluation covers human action recognition and closed-loop delivery and retrieval. In the privacy-preserving curtain setting, mmHRI achieves 85.09% action-recognition accuracy, outperforming existing radar-based alternatives. Robot trials further demonstrate successful delivery and retrieval under visual occlusion, with stable task performance across unseen subjects, clutter configurations, and environments.

</details>


### [119] [Proprioceptive Force Estimation for Quadruped Locomotion and Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34222)
*Run Wang,Xu Yang,Alapati Tuerxun,Yilin Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究本体感觉历史推断的三维力估计能否同时支持负载承载与牵引引导任务，通过联合训练估计器与运动策略，在仿真与Unitree Go1实机上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动过程中需承受负载力，同时牵引力可用于指定期望运动。作者希望探究一个共享的三维力估计（以牛顿为单位、由持续负载下的本体感觉历史推断）能否同时支撑这两种任务，避免为不同任务分别设计估计与策略。

Method: 联合训练力估计器与运动策略，采用监督的力与速度输出以及学习到的潜在上下文。估计出的力既用于条件化运动策略，又通过解析映射生成平面速度与偏航角速度指令以实现牵引引导。

Result: 在持续力仿真扫描中，各分量力均方根误差的时间均值范围为1.44至2.83 N。与域随机化基线相比，框架将速度跟踪与基座姿态误差分数分别降低21.6%与46.5%，并将平均存活率从68.29%提升至94.60%。Unitree Go1实验展示了静止垂直与水平力估计、承载超70 N训练力上限的8.5 kg负载运动，以及使用同一力估计接口的牵引引导。

Conclusion: 共享的三维力估计接口能够同时支持负载承载与牵引引导，在仿真和实机上均优于基线，且具备超越训练力范围的泛化能力。

Abstract: Payload forces must be accommodated during locomotion, while leash forces can specify desired motion. We investigate whether a shared three-dimensional force estimate in newtons, inferred from proprioceptive history under sustained loading, can support both tasks. An estimator and locomotion policy are jointly trained with supervised force and velocity outputs and learned latent context. The estimated force conditions locomotion and additionally generates planar-velocity and yaw-rate commands for leash guidance through an analytical map. In sustained-force simulation sweeps, temporal means of componentwise force root mean square error range from 1.44 to 2.83\,N. Compared with a domain-randomized baseline, the framework reduces velocity-tracking and base-orientation error scores by 21.6\% and 46.5\%, respectively, and increases mean survival from 68.29\% to 94.60\% in separate sustained-force tests. Unitree Go1 experiments demonstrate stationary vertical and horizontal force estimation, locomotion with an 8.5\,kg payload whose weight exceeds the 70\,N training force limit, and leash guidance using the same force-estimation interface.

</details>


### [120] [GAE: General Action Expert for Real-Time Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34233)
*Yuefan Wang,Huaicheng Zhou,Xiao He,Zhijie He,Mingchuan Yang,Huayi Zhang,Li Chai,Jinxin Liu,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GAE统一学习框架，实现通用、低延迟的人形机器人全身遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形化身需通过遥操作实现多样动态的全身行为，同时保持人机同步响应，现有系统难以兼顾通用性与低延迟。

Method: 构建多源大规模人体运动数据集（视频、动画、动捕）并标准化增强；采用两阶段训练：特权生成器策略在仿真中跟踪人体运动并生成可行轨迹，可部署执行器策略在课程域随机化下学习跟踪；引入延迟条件预判机制自适应补偿端到端延迟。

Result: 在Unitree G1与Westlake O1机器人上的仿真和真实实验表明，系统能平滑模仿多样、敏捷且富有表现力的人体行为。

Conclusion: GAE为通用低延迟人形全身遥操作提供了有效统一方案。

Abstract: Humanoid avatars extend human physical presence beyond the body, enabling people to participate in social, service, and labor activities through remotely operated robots. This requires teleoperation systems capable of realizing diverse and dynamic whole-body behaviors while maintaining responsive human-robot synchronization. We present General Action Expert(GAE), a unified learning framework for general-purpose, low-latency humanoid whole-body teleoperation. To cover diverse human behaviors, GAE builds a large-scale human motion dataset from heterogeneous sources, including videos, animations, and motion capture, followed by standardization and augmentation. GAE then addresses the noise and embodiment mismatch in human motions with a two-stage training paradigm: a privileged generator policy first tracks human motion references in simulation and rolls out feasible humanoid trajectories; a deployable executor policy then learns to track these generated trajectories under curriculum domain randomization. For responsive human-robot synchronization, GAE introduces a latency-conditioned anticipation mechanism that adaptively compensates for end-to-end delay during real-time teleoperation. Simulation and real-world experiments on Unitree G1 and Westlake O1 robots demonstrate that GAE enables humanoids to smoothly mirror diverse, agile, and expressive human behaviors. Project website: https://wangyf0928.github.io/gae-wlrobotics/

</details>


### [121] [WAM-OPD: Sharpening World Action Models via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34250)
*Panjun Liu,Xiaohan Lei,Shiqi Zhang,Yikun Wang,Yongxin Zhang,Mingyi Hu,Shida Sun,Jiateng Shou,Wengang Zhou,Jiajun Deng,Zhiwei Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 WAM-OPD，将在线策略蒸馏（OPD）引入预训练世界动作模型（WAM），并用前缀加权轨迹回放（PWTR）避免蒸馏过程中反复采集环境数据，实现无需额外环境交互的任务适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练世界动作模型（WAM）具备跨任务的通用操作能力，但想在目标任务上达到专家水平，同时不损害预训练技能，仍然十分困难。直接拟合目标任务的窄分布会损失通用性，而 OPD 虽能在学生自身分布下迁移教师知识，却需要不断的环境 rollout 以保持 on-policy，这在仿真中昂贵、在真机上往往不可行。

Method: 提出 WAM-OPD，把 on-policy distillation 用于 WAM。为免去蒸馏期间的重复环境 rollout，设计 prefix-weighted trajectory replay（PWTR）：使用固定轨迹池（以初始学生 rollout 为主，辅以任务专用教师 rollout 扩大覆盖）；对每条回放轨迹，令当前策略以连续存储的历史为条件生成新的去噪路径，由任务专用教师提供监督。由于去噪路径随策略更新而刷新、但环境轨迹固定，会产生历史分布偏移；PWTR 用基于每个决策之前轨迹前缀累计路径得分得到的代理重要性权重，对每个决策的蒸馏损失重新加权来缓解该偏移。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，蒸馏过程中无需额外环境交互即可完成目标任务适应；在两类设置下，WAM-OPD 均提升目标任务的性能，同时在未参与适应的任务上保持接近初始模型的表现。

Conclusion: WAM-OPD 证明 OPD 可有效迁移到世界动作模型，配合 PWTR 在不增加环境交互的前提下实现目标任务的专家级适应，并较好保留预训练的通用技能，具备仿真与真机可用性。

Abstract: Pretrained world action models (WAMs) provide generalist capabilities across diverse robotic manipulation tasks, yet improving target-task performance to an expert level without degrading pretrained skills remains challenging. We explore on-policy distillation (OPD) for WAMs and introduce WAM-OPD. WAM-OPD inherits the advantage of OPD methods that transfer task-specific teacher knowledge under the student's own induced distribution, rather than directly fitting the student to a narrow task-specific data distribution. However, in closed-loop manipulation, the observation histories change as the student policy evolves, requiring fresh environment rollouts to remain on-policy. Applying OPD to WAMs entails repeated data collection, which is costly even in simulation and often impractical on real robots. To avoid repeated environment rollouts during distillation, we introduce prefix-weighted trajectory replay (PWTR). PWTR uses a fixed trajectory pool composed primarily of initial-student rollouts, supplemented with task-specific teacher rollouts to broaden trajectory coverage. For each trajectory replayed from this pool, PWTR conditions the current policy on successive stored histories to generate fresh denoising paths, along which the task-specific teacher provides supervision. Although these denoising paths are refreshed as the policy evolves, the replayed environment trajectories remain fixed. PWTR therefore reweights per-decision distillation losses using proxy importance weights derived from path scores accumulated over the trajectory prefix preceding each decision to mitigate the resulting shift in the history distribution. Simulated and real-world experiments demonstrate task adaptation without additional environment interaction during distillation. In both settings, WAM-OPD improves target-task performance while retaining near-initial performance on tasks excluded from adaptation.

</details>


### [122] [UMR: Universal Manipulation Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34256)
*Song Liu,Linyi Li,Yanshun Zhao,Rxuan Li,Xinrui Xu,Yi Ju,Yahui Deng,Senge Zhang,Guoyu Liu,Yixuan Li,Wuyang Zhang,Yao Li,Congcong Zhu,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出通用操作表示UMR及策略WEPVLA，实现从人类演示到异构机器人的零样本技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略依赖特定本体的动作空间，难以大规模利用跨本体演示，且迁移到新本体和空间变化受限。

Method: UMR将操作分解为与本体无关的World Flow（世界坐标下任务相关物体运动）和Ego Trajectory（相对当前位姿的末端执行器运动），以WEPVLA（0.5B参数）实现，含双流Point Action Adapter与统一Point Action Expert，并用SE(3)共轭耦合两部分；辅以数据高效策略DES，通过阶段感知点云编辑多样化物体配置并保持接触几何。

Result: 仿真中LIBERO平均成功率97.5%，RLBench 10任务85.7%；真实实验中每任务约10分钟人类演示、无机器人演示，零样本迁移在六种评估设置下平均成功率91.7%，对比HumanEgo的60.8%。

Conclusion: UMR与WEPVLA提供统一几何动作空间，显著提升跨本体零样本迁移与数据效率，验证了从人类演示直接迁移到异构机器人的可行性。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied manipulation hinges on a unified action representation that generalizes across embodiments and scales readily. Yet existing policies rely on embodiment-specific action spaces, making cross-embodiment demonstrations difficult to leverage at scale and limiting transfer to new embodiments and spatial variations. To this end, we introduce Universal Manipulation Representation (UMR), a unified action representation that enables zero-shot skill transfer from human demonstrations to heterogeneous robots. UMR decomposes manipulation into two functionally distinct yet geometrically linked components: embodiment-agnostic World Flow, which describes task-relevant object motion in the world frame, and Ego Trajectory, which represents end-effector motion relative to the current pose. We instantiate UMR as World--Ego Point VLA (WEPVLA), a compact 0.5B-parameter policy that learns in the unified geometric action space through a dual-stream Point Action Adapter and a unified Point Action Expert, with an $SE(3)$ conjugation coupling the two components. To improve data efficiency, we complement UMR with a Data-Efficient Strategy (DES) that diversifies object configurations through stage-aware point-cloud editing while preserving demonstrated contact geometry. In simulation, WEPVLA achieves average success rates of 97.5\% on LIBERO and 85.7\% on the 10-task RLBench benchmark. In real-world experiments, a single policy trained on human demonstrations augmented by DES transfers zero-shot to diverse deployment conditions. With about 10 minutes of collected human demonstrations per task and no robot demonstrations, it achieves 91.7\% average success across six evaluation settings, compared with 60.8\% for HumanEgo. Code and additional materials are available at https://umr-wepvla.github.io/.

</details>


### [123] [RoboICL: Embodied In-Context Learning with GPT-6 Astra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34261)
*Fangcheng Liu,Yeqing Shen,Anda Cheng,Weishi Mi,Chao Tang,Chenyuan Liu,Yushun Xiang,Tingguang Li,Yong-Lu Li,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboICL 是一个上下文学习机器人控制框架，通过分离演示上下文与交互记忆，无需机器人专属参数更新或学习型 VLA，即在通用视觉语言模型上显著提升高精度与长程任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉语言模型（如 GPT-Astra）在零样本机器人控制上表现优异，但在高精度和长程任务上明显较弱。作者希望在不进行机器人专属参数更新、也不训练新 VLA 的前提下缩小这一差距。

Method: 提出 RoboICL 上下文机器人控制框架，将"演示上下文"（可用时提供录制示例）与"交互记忆"（累积模型自身动作与观察结果）分离；两者共享 observation–action–receipt–observation 语法。为跨任务阶段保留经验，RoboICL 将采样演示块与有界锚定记忆结合：固定锚点保持早期 rollout 交互可用于上下文学习，最新交互支持即时纠错。可选 Jev 门控动作复用可减少模型调用。

Result: 在 30 个 RoboDojo 任务上（Open 用零样本，其余用一个演示），RoboICL 相较官方零样本 GPT-Astra 在每个类别提升 20–27 个进度分。在 Memory 与 Open 上领先排行榜基线，Precision 与最强基线相当，Long-Horizon 保持竞争力。30 任务 Overall 得分 50.64，最强基线 33.68。在十任务子集上得分 60.60，与 π0.5 + GPT-Astra 混合方法差距在 2.00 分内。三个真实机器人任务上，平均进度从零样本 14.45 提升至单样本 63.33、三样本 78.89。在两个开发任务上，可选的 Jev 门控动作复用将 GPT-Astra 调用减少 33–48%。

Conclusion: RoboICL 证明了无需机器人专属微调或学习型 VLA，仅通过精心设计的上下文与记忆机制即可显著增强通用视觉语言模型在机器人控制上的精度与长程能力，并在仿真与真实机器人上均获得一致提升。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language models offer a promising way to zero-shot robot control: \gptastra{} excels at open-ended and language- or image-conditioned manipulation but remains substantially weaker on high-precision and long-horizon tasks. We introduce \emph{RoboICL}, an in-context robot-control framework that narrows these gaps without robot-specific parameter updates or a learned VLA. RoboICL separates \emph{demonstration context}, which provides recorded examples when available, from \emph{interaction memory}, which accumulates the model's own actions and observed outcomes. Both use a shared observation--action--receipt--observation grammar. To preserve experience across task stages, RoboICL combines sampled demonstration blocks with bounded anchored memory. Fixed anchors keep earlier rollout interactions available for in-context learning, while the latest interaction supports immediate error correction. Across 30 RoboDojo tasks, using zero shot for Open and one demonstration elsewhere, RoboICL improves on official zero-shot \gptastra{} by 20--27 progress-score points in every category. It leads the leaderboard baselines on Memory and Open, achieves comparable performance to the strongest Precision baseline, and remains competitive on Long-Horizon. Its 30-task Overall score is 50.64, versus 33.68 for the strongest baseline. On a separate ten-task subset, RoboICL scores 60.60, within 2.00 points of the $π_{0.5}$ + \gptastra{} hybrid approach. On three real-robot tasks, mean progress rises from 14.45 at zero shot to 63.33 at one shot and 78.89 at three shots. On two development tasks, optional Jev-gated action reuse reduces \gptastra{} calls by 33--48\%. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Mosi-AI/RoboICL}{https://github.com/Mosi-AI/RoboICL}.

</details>


### [124] [SAGE: Symbolic Action-Gating and Editing for LLM Task Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34268)
*Trung Minh Bui,JongSul Moon,YoungOuk Kim,Quang-Ngoc Phung,Se-Woong Jun,Dongin Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE是一个单LLM规划器，通过符号门控（运行时安全监控）与局部编辑（仅重生成失败子目标后缀）两个轻量机制，在无泄漏协议下评测，实现更可靠恢复、更低LLM调用成本，并可作为近乎零开销的边缘安全层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为具身家用智能体的认知核心，但其计划在执行前很少对照真实环境模型做校验；同时现有基准过于饱和，无法区分不同方法的优劣，导致任务成功率报告失真。

Method: 提出SAGE（Symbolic Action-Gating and Editing）：1）领域无关符号门，约250行Python、零token、O(|π|)复杂度，以带类型理由阻断违反前置条件的动作，作为运行时安全监控；2）局部编辑，仅重生成失败子目标的后缀，保留已完成与未受影响的工作；3）混合种子+实时记忆库支持冷启动覆盖。评测采用无泄漏协议（留一检索），覆盖5个开放权重模型与75任务AI2-THOR基准。

Result: 标准基准目标完整性饱和（52%实例可被平凡解决），SAGE与强分层基线持平；在更难的方法无关多目标组合上，SAGE完整性领先重新显现（四个模型上+0.06至+0.23）。注入执行中期故障时，SAGE恢复可靠性媲美整计划重规划器，LLM调用减少2.4-3.3倍。符号监控在动作执行前阻断不安全动作，使所有被测规划器的模拟器步成功率提升（最高+0.11），且该信号为验证器不可见（非循环）。门控不调用任何模型（0.008 ms/计划），可在Jetson AGX Orin上近乎免费运行。

Conclusion: SAGE以极低成本实现了可验证、可恢复且安全的LLM规划，并公开基准、无泄漏协议、恢复与安全门控测试工具及验证器可移植性研究（ALFWorld自动诱导，留出集0.89）。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now the default cognitive core of embodied household agents, yet the plans they emit are rarely checked against a grounded model of the environment before execution, and the task-success they report is often measured on benchmarks so saturated that no method can be separated from another. We present SAGE (Symbolic Action-Gating and Editing), a single-LLM planner built from two lightweight mechanisms: a domain-agnostic symbolic gate (~250 lines of Python, zero tokens, $O(|π|)$) that blocks precondition-violating actions with typed reasons as a runtime safety monitor, and a local edit that regenerates only the failed sub-goal's suffix, keeping completed and untouched work intact; a hybrid seed+live memory store supports cold-start coverage. We evaluate under a leak-free protocol (leave-one-out retrieval) over five open-weight models and a 75-task AI2-THOR benchmark. On the standard benchmark goal-completeness saturates (52% of instances trivially solved) and SAGE ties strong hierarchical baselines. On a harder, method-agnostic multi-goal composition, SAGE's completeness lead re-emerges large (+0.06 to +0.23 across four models). Under injected mid-execution failures, SAGE recovers as reliably as whole-plan replanners at 2.4-3.3x fewer LLM calls. As a verify-before-execute gate, the symbolic monitor blocks unsafe actions before actuation and raises simulator-reported step-success for every planner tested (up to +0.11), a signal the verifier never sees (non-circular). Because the gate calls no model (0.008 ms/plan), it is a safety layer that runs essentially free on the edge: SAGE planning reproduces its quality on a Jetson AGX Orin, where small-model verification helps most. We release the benchmark, the leak-free protocol, the recovery and safety-gate harnesses, and a verifier-portability study (auto-induced on ALFWorld, 0.89 held-out).

</details>


### [125] [Bayesian Active Learning for Intent Disambiguation in Interactive Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34270)
*Huao Li,Carson Sobolewski,Augustinos Saravanos,William Tan,John Karigiannis,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种贝叶斯框架，将澄清过程视为对基于STL任务规范的主动学习，用LLM初始化候选规范并生成澄清问题，贝叶斯优化估计用户意图不确定性，最终由形式化规划器生成可验证轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言指令常存在歧义、不完整或欠规范问题，机器人需要推断并执行人类意图。尽管LLM提供了强大的澄清接口，但依赖生成模型驱动多轮对话可能引入系统性失败，因此需要更可靠的澄清机制。

Method: 将澄清建模为对grounded STL任务规范的主动学习问题：用LLM初始化候选形式化规范并将有信息量的对比转化为自然语言澄清问题；用贝叶斯优化维护对用户意图的不确定性估计，并选择最大化信息增益的查询；收敛后将推断出的STL规范交给形式化规划器合成可验证的机器人轨迹。

Result: 在四个仿真和真实世界任务域中，该方法总体取得更高的任务满足度，并且相比LLM基线需要更少的澄清轮次，同时帮助较小模型缩小与更大推理模型之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 将贝叶斯主动学习与LLM澄清结合，可提升机器人任务推断的可靠性与效率，并增强可验证性，同时降低对大型模型能力的依赖。

Abstract: Interactive robot planning requires robots to infer and execute human intentions from natural language instructions that are often ambiguous, incomplete, or underspecified. Although large language models (LLMs) provide a powerful interface for clarification, relying on the generative model to drive an multi-turn conversation can introduce systematic failures. We propose a Bayesian framework that treats clarification as an active learning problem over grounded Signal Temporal Logic (STL) task specifications. Our method uses LLMs to initialize candidate formal specifications and translate informative contrasts into natural-language clarification questions, while Bayesian optimization maintains uncertainty estimation over user intent and selects queries that maximize information gain. After convergence, the inferred STL specification is passed to a formal planner to synthesize a verifiable robot trajectory. Across four simulated and real-world task domains, our approach generally achieves higher task satisfaction and requires fewer clarification rounds than LLM baselines, while helping smaller models close the performance gap against larger reasoning models.

</details>


### [126] [NavHarness: Towards Lifelong Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34276)
*Xunyi Zhao,Jian Zhou,Sihao Lin,Gengze Zhou,Zerui Li,Xinyu Yan,Jiajun Liu,Anton van den Hengel,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 NavHarness——一个免训练的具身导航框架，将记忆处理嵌入导航循环，实现跨任务的终身导航，并在 GOAT-Bench 等基准上取得显著提升与最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型虽能通过多轮多模态推理完成单个具身导航任务，但在连续任务中，智能体必须依赖不断更新的地图与历史搜索记录，而这些信息可能不完整或与新观测冲突。因此，如何让多轮推理会话在经验上层层累积，是迈向终身导航的关键问题。

Method: NavHarness 是一个免训练的具身导航“harness”：其多轮智能体会话综合地图、任务记录与房屋知识，并与观测进行核对、记录修正以指导行动；它在新的对话中保留这些经验用于新任务或恢复尝试，并通过结果验证与运行结束总结支持后续复用；同时在多房屋部署中进行经验整合。

Result: 在 GOAT-Bench 上，相比仅用上下文的独立会话，NavHarness 使 s-SR 分别提升 18.6 个百分点（Astra）和 22.6 个百分点（Opus 5）。使用 SLAM 估计位姿时，配合 GPT-6 Astra 达到最优表现：GOAT-Bench 上 83.7 s-SR 与 36.9 e-SR，IR2R-CE 上 85.9 s-SR。分析表明结构化恢复交接优于等长摘要。

Conclusion: 终身导航的进展不仅取决于单任务能力的提升，更取决于连续的推理会话如何建立在先前经验之上；经验整合（consolidation）超越了单纯保留地图与任务记录的效果。

Abstract: Frontier models can now perform well on individual embodied navigation tasks through multi-round multimodal reasoning with simple tools. Across successive tasks, however, an agent must also rely on an evolving map and earlier search records, both of which may be incomplete or conflict with new observations. We present NavHarness, a training-free embodied harness towards lifelong navigation that makes memory processing part of the navigation loop. During navigation, its multi-round agentic session draws on maps, task records, and house knowledge, checking them against observations and recording corrections to guide its actions. NavHarness preserves this experience across fresh conversations for new tasks or recovery attempts, while outcome verification and run-end summaries support its later reuse. On GOAT-Bench, NavHarness improves s-SR over context-only independent sessions by 18.6 points with Astra and 22.6 with Opus 5. Using SLAM-estimated poses, NavHarness with GPT-6 Astra achieves state-of-the-art task success of 83.7 s-SR with 36.9 e-SR on GOAT-Bench and 85.9 s-SR on IR2R-CE. To understand these gains, we examine how experience is carried between sessions and find that structured recovery handovers outperform length-matched summaries. In extended deployments across houses, consolidation improves navigation beyond retaining maps and task records, with case studies showing how agents use earlier experience to interpret new goals, investigate unresolved questions, and resume failed searches. We suggest that progress towards lifelong navigation depends on how successive reasoning sessions build on prior experience, alongside improvements in single-task capability.

</details>


### [127] [TLC-DiT: Task-Aligned Local Visual Conditioning for Robust Multitask Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34297)
*Xianbo Cai,Hideyuki Ichiwara,Zihang Wang,Yijun Lu,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TLC-DiT 在 Multitask Diffusion Transformer 策略上加入显式的任务引导局部视觉特征图，不改变扩散目标与动作生成过程，在 LIBERO、LIBERO-plus 及真实双臂任务上取得提升，并提供可视化的视觉证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言条件机器人策略在多任务操作上已有进展，但任务相关的局部视觉证据通常隐藏在视觉骨干或注意力层内部，导致策略难以检查，且在视觉变化下表现脆弱。作者认为这反映了缺少显式、与任务对齐的局部视觉通道。

Method: 提出 TLC-DiT，作为 Multitask DiT 策略的即插即用扩展。对每个相机视角，用 CLIP 任务嵌入通过 FiLM 调制冻结的 DINOv2 patch 特征，再由轻量 CoordConv CNN 适配器精炼为平滑空间图，并与原始全局图像、语言、关节状态和时间步条件拼接。

Result: LIBERO 上平均成功率 93.5%，高于 Multitask DiT 的 86.5% 与 SmolVLA 的 79.25%。LIBERO-plus 总成功率由 54.07% 提升到 57.24%，在相机、背景和传感器噪声变化下增益更大。真实双臂任务中，Teabag Putting 完成率由 44% 提升至 89%，Match Box Opening 性能相当。特征图可视化显示模型在不同视角和扰动下关注任务相关区域。

Conclusion: TLC-DiT 通过显式任务对齐的局部视觉通道，在保持原有扩散目标与动作生成不变的前提下，同时提升了策略性能、视觉鲁棒性与可解释性。

Abstract: Language-conditioned robot policies have made clear progress in multitask manipulation, but task-relevant local visual evidence usually stays hidden inside a visual backbone or attention layers. This leaves the policy difficult to inspect and fragile under visual change, two symptoms of a missing explicit, task-aligned local visual channel. We present TLC-DiT, a plug-in extension of the Multitask Diffusion Transformer (DiT) policy that adds explicit task-guided local visual feature maps without changing the diffusion objective or the action-generation process. For each camera view, frozen DINOv2 patch features are modulated by the CLIP task embedding through FiLM and refined by a lightweight CoordConv CNN adapter into smooth spatial maps, which are concatenated with the original global image, language, joint-state, and timestep conditions. On LIBERO, TLC-DiT reaches a 93.5% average success rate, compared with 86.5% for Multitask DiT and 79.25% for SmolVLA. On LIBERO-plus, the total success rate improves from 54.07% to 57.24%, with larger gains under camera, background, and sensor-noise changes. In real-world bimanual tasks, TLC-DiT raises Teabag Putting completion from 44% to 89% while maintaining comparable Match Box Opening performance. Feature-map visualizations confirm that the model attends to task-relevant regions across views and perturbations, providing a direct way to inspect the visual evidence.

</details>


### [128] [When World Models Lie: Adaptive Safety Analysis Under Wrong Imaginations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34300)
*John Cao,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应潜空间安全滤波器，利用直接观测到的世界模型误差进行校准，通过自适应保形推断构建在线不确定性集并以悲观方式评估安全性，在仿真和硬件实验中显著减少失效同时保持任务完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型为高维机器人系统的安全推理提供了强大基础，但其预测可能存在偏差、校准不良或自信地出错。潜空间安全滤波器常在基于世界模型动力学学习的Hamilton-Jacobi安全值函数上运行，若世界模型不正确，值函数会继承其误差并产生过度自信的安全估计。现有潜空间安全滤波器依赖集成分歧或值目标一致性残差等辅助信号进行自适应，但这些信号在世界模型预测偏离观测时可能仍然很小。

Method: 提出一种自适应潜空间安全滤波器，使用直接观测到的世界模型误差来校准安全推理。方法利用自适应保形推断（Adaptive Conformal Inference），从预测潜状态与观测推断潜状态之间的差异构建在线不确定性集，然后通过在这些集合上最小化已学习的值函数，以悲观方式进行安全性评估。并提供了自适应不确定性半径的有限时间覆盖保证。

Result: 通过仿真和硬件实验表明，与最先进的潜空间安全滤波器相比，该方法显著减少了失效，同时保持了任务完成率。

Conclusion: 直接利用观测到的世界模型误差并借助自适应保形推断，可在世界模型准确时保持最小保守性，在观测揭示模型失配时变得更加谨慎，从而在潜空间安全滤波中实现更好的安全性与任务完成平衡，并具备有限时间覆盖保证。

Abstract: World models offer a powerful substrate for safety reasoning in high-dimensional robotic systems, but they are also fallible: their predictions can be biased, miscalibrated, or confidently wrong. This creates a central challenge for latent-space safety filters, which often learn Hamilton-Jacobi safety value functions on the dynamics of a world model. If the world model is incorrect, the resulting value function can inherit its errors and produce overconfident safety estimates. Existing latent safety filters often rely on auxiliary signals such as ensemble disagreement or value-target consistency residuals for adaptation, but these signals can remain small even when the world model's predictions deviate from observations. We propose an adaptive latent safety filter that calibrates safety reasoning using directly observed world-model error. Our method uses Adaptive Conformal Inference to construct online uncertainty sets from discrepancies between predicted and observation-inferred latent states, then evaluates safety pessimistically by minimizing the learned value function over these sets. This allows the filter to remain minimally conservative when the world model is accurate, while becoming more cautious when observations reveal model mismatch. We provide a finite-time coverage guarantee for the adaptive uncertainty radius. Through simulation and hardware experiments, we show that our method significantly reduces failures relative to state-of-the-art latent safety filters while preserving task completion.

</details>


### [129] [Predictive Semantic Safety: From Visual Physical Reasoning to Safety-Critical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34356)
*Taekyung Kim,Salem Fradi,Yanning Dai,Mateusz Ostaszewski,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出预测性语义安全（PSS）框架，将视觉物理推理与基于后备的安全过滤结合，用VLM预测物理事件与位移，经运动模型、共形预测和几何转换得到预测占用，从而在MuJoCo中实现99.3%安全率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人当前几何环境无法体现物理交互可能引发的未来危险，仅依赖当前障碍几何的安全方法不足。

Method: VLM预测物理事件及时机或直接预测物体位移；显式运动模型将事件假设转为轨迹；拆分共形预测校准位置误差，几何形状边界转为预测占用；评估指定后备机动并推导输入仿射约束，在保证后备可行性的同时最小修改名义输入。

Result: MuJoCo中Unitree Go1实验涵盖坠落装置、冲击导致支撑丧失和接触传播，PSS安全回合率达99.3%，而仅用当前障碍几何的后备控制屏障函数基线为43.3%。

Conclusion: 结合视觉物理推理与基于后备的安全过滤可显著提升动态物理危险场景下的机器人安全性。

Abstract: Physical interactions can create future hazards that are not apparent from the robot's current geometric surroundings. We present a framework termed Predictive Semantic Safety (PSS), which connects visual physical reasoning to backup-based safety filtering. A vision-language model (VLM) predicts physical events and their timing or directly predicts object displacements. An explicit motion model converts event hypotheses into object trajectories. Split conformal prediction calibrates position errors jointly across specified objects, observation times, and future times; geometric shape bounds convert the resulting position regions into predicted object occupancy. PSS evaluates a prescribed backup maneuver against this occupancy and derives input-affine constraints for minimally modifying the nominal input while preserving backup feasibility under the robot dynamics and input limits. MuJoCo experiments with a Unitree Go1 consider falling fixtures, impact-driven support loss, and contact propagation. PSS achieves a safe episode rate of 99.3%, compared with 43.3% for a Backup Control Barrier Function baseline that only uses current obstacle geometry.

</details>


### [130] [FutureDuet: Decoupling Observation Access from Future Supervision in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34362)
*Jie Wu,Yuzhi Huang,Junqi Liu,Weichen Zhang,Haibin Huang,Yin Chen,Jingyan Jiang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FutureDuet 为机器人策略中的主视角与腕部视角分别设计不同的未来预测目标，提升交互精度而不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型将主相机与腕部相机观测融合为单一视觉流并用相同的未来视频目标训练，但两者的视觉动态本质不同：主相机稳定，反映场景级任务演化；腕部相机随末端执行器运动，混合了局部交互变化、视角偏移与自遮挡。这些不同的预测需求提示两个视角应采用不同的未来目标。

Method: FutureDuet 在保留双视角用于控制的同时，为每条视觉流设置不同的未来目标：主视角用未来 RGB 建模任务演化，并以交互掩码和机器人骨架将监督聚焦于任务物体与机器人运动；腕部流用未来隐空间预测建模短时程交互变化，无需像素级重建。ActionDiT 联合读取由此得到的 Task State 与 Interaction State，融合场景级进度与近距离交互证据。所有辅助预测模块仅在训练时使用，不增加推理开销。

Result: 在 RoboTwin50 上取得 94.2% 干净场景与 94.1% 随机化场景成功率，在 LIBERO 上平均成功率达 99.2%。提升在六个需要精确交互的 RoboTwin50 任务上最显著，相比 Fast-WAM 在干净与随机化设置下分别平均提升 9.2% 与 12.8%。

Conclusion: 对照研究进一步表明，分离腕部通路以及为主、腕两视角分别设计未来监督可带来互补增益，验证了差异化未来目标设计的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot action generation with future visual supervision. Existing WAMs commonly fuse main and wrist observations into one visual stream and train both with the same future-video objective, despite their different visual dynamics. A stable main camera reveals scene-level task evolution, whereas wrist cameras move with the end effector, mixing local interaction changes with viewpoint shifts and self-occlusion. These contrasting predictive demands suggest that the two views may benefit from different future objectives. We introduce FutureDuet, which retains both views for control, while allowing each visual stream to receive a different future objective. For the main view, future RGB models task evolution, while interaction masks and robot skeletons focus supervision on task objects and robot motion. For the wrist stream, future latent prediction models short-horizon interaction changes without requiring pixel-level reconstruction. ActionDiT jointly reads the resulting Task State and Interaction State, combining scene-level progress with close-range interaction evidence. All auxiliary prediction modules are training-only, adding no inference overhead. FutureDuet achieves 94.2% clean and 94.1% randomized success on RoboTwin50 and 99.2% average success on LIBERO. The improvements are most pronounced on six RoboTwin50 tasks that require precise interaction, averaging gains of 9.2% and 12.8% over Fast-WAM in clean and randomized settings. Controlled studies further show complementary gains from separating the wrist pathway and designing future supervision separately for the two views.

</details>


### [131] [RoboIRGBench: Benchmarking Implicit Referential Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34384)
*Aernaer Akelijiang,Jiannan Li,Zhineng Chen,Jingjing Chen,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究隐式指称定位（IRG）能力，提出RoboIRG-Bench基准，发现VLA模型在隐式指令下存在显著鲁棒性差距，并强调需要更强的多模态整合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型基准通常假设任务相关信息在指令中被明确指定，但现实中人类常用隐式指称（涉及对象、数量、关系），要求机器人从语言和感知语境中恢复目标。这一能力（IRG）尚未被系统评估。

Method: 基于RoboMME构建RoboIRG-Bench，包含来自11个任务的40个变体，涵盖四类挑战：直接、推理中介、空间和语境指称定位。评估了具有不同记忆机制的代表性VLA模型，并在Franka Research 3机械臂上进行真实世界验证。

Result: 发现明显的指称鲁棒性差距：显式指令下表现良好的模型在需从语境恢复信息时性能急剧下降；推理中介和空间指称尤为困难；使用外部VLM的模型鲁棒性更好但仍存在显著失败；替换更强VLM不能消除差距。真实机器人实验中间隙依然存在，表现为错误指称定位和下游执行失败。

Conclusion: IRG是可靠机器人指令跟随中一个独特且未被充分探索的能力，需要能稳健整合语言、感知、推理和行动的VLA模型。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, yet existing benchmarks typically assume that task-relevant information is explicitly specified in the instruction. In practice, however, humans frequently refer to objects, quantities, and relations implicitly, requiring robots to recover the intended target from linguistic and perceptual context. We study this capability as Implicit Referential Grounding (IRG) and introduce RoboIRG-Bench, a manipulation benchmark designed to systematically evaluate it. Built upon RoboMME, RoboIRG-Bench contains 40 variants derived from 11 tasks and covers four challenges, including direct, reasoning-mediated, spatial, and contextual referential grounding. As IRG often requires retaining and retrieving previously established context, we evaluate representative VLAs spanning different memory mechanisms. Our evaluation reveals a noticeable referential robustness gap. Models that perform well under explicit instructions can degrade sharply when the same task-relevant information must be recovered from context. Reasoning-mediated and spatial references are particularly challenging, while models using external VLMs show greater robustness but still exhibit significant failures. Moreover, replacing the external VLM with a stronger model does not eliminate these gaps. We further validate these findings on a Franka Research 3 robot arm, where the gap persists under real-world manipulation and manifests as both incorrect referent grounding and downstream execution failures. These results establish IRG as a distinct and underexplored capability for reliable robotic instruction following and highlight the need for VLAs that can robustly integrate language, perception, reasoning, and action.

</details>


### [132] [Contact-Aware Impedance Controller for Robot-Assisted Ultrasound Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34400)
*MD Miraj Arefin,M Efe Tiryaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于B超图像的接触感知方法与接触感知阻抗控制器，实现无测力传感器的接触检测与定位，提升机器人超声成像的安全性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助超声成像需要可靠控制器来检测并定位探头—组织交互。传统方法依赖力测量，存在残余力偏置等问题，因此希望利用超声图像本身作为直接接触反馈。

Method: 提出基于B型超声图像的接触感知方法，独立于力测量检测声学接触，从而实现接触条件下的力/力矩清零以减小残余力偏置；接触期间沿曲面探头连续估计有效接触位置，并据此更新控制器的交互坐标系，实现物理探头—组织接触点的视觉伺服；并设计接触感知阻抗控制器。

Result: 在琼脂体模上实验，探头滚转角从-15°到15°范围内接触定位RMSE为1.46±0.14 mm；静态滚动时任务空间跟踪精度与传统固定坐标系阻抗控制器相当，同时最大压缩交互力从31.56 N降至20.09 N，降低36.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明超声图像可作为直接接触反馈，用于安全且精确的机器人辅助超声成像，具有良好应用潜力。

Abstract: Safe robot-assisted ultrasound imaging requires a reliable controller able to detect and localize probe--tissue interaction. In this paper, we present a B-mode ultrasound image-based contact perception method and a contact-aware impedance controller for robotic ultrasound imaging. The proposed method detects acoustic contact independently of force measurements, enabling contact-conditioned force/torque taring to reduce residual wrench bias. During contact, the method continuously estimates the effective contact location along the curved probe surface and uses it to update the controller interaction frame, enabling visual servoing of the physical probe--tissue contact point during imaging. Experiments on an agar phantom demonstrated a contact-localization RMSE of $\mathbf{1.46 \pm 0.14}$~mm over probe roll angles from $\mathbf{-15^\circ}$ to $\mathbf{15^\circ}$. During static rolling, the proposed controller maintained task-space tracking accuracy comparable to a conventional fixed-frame impedance controller while reducing the maximum compressive interaction force from $\mathbf{31.56}$~N to $\mathbf{20.09}$~N, corresponding to a $\mathbf{36.3\%}$ reduction. These results demonstrate the potential of ultrasound images as direct contact feedback for safe and accurate robot-assisted ultrasound imaging.

</details>


### [133] [From Language to Task Maps: Compiling Semantic Relations While Preserving Task-Relevant Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34412)
*Jaegyun Park,Jingwang Lee,Jungsoo Lee,Soonwoong Hwang,Wansoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"类型化语义-几何接口"，让语言只负责指定实体、关系与阶段，由机器人端编译器用共形几何代数构造关系专属任务映射与微分，并通过RMPflow组合成连续目标，从而避免把定性关系展开成完整位姿时引入的意外约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言操作指令表达的是定性关系（如"在……上面""靠近"），而连续控制器需要可求值、可微、有完成条件的任务量。把定性关系强行展开为完整位姿会补全未指定的相对构型，从而引入非预期的额外约束。作者希望明确划分语言模型与编译器各自的责任，使语言保持定性、几何由编译过程补全且不越界。

Method: 提出类型化的语义-几何接口：语言侧只给出实体、关系与阶段，每个关系索引一份注册规范，说明其任务相关区分与需保留的自由度；机器人端编译器将这些规范落到几何上，用共形几何代数构建关系专属任务映射与相容微分，再用RMPflow把各策略组合起来。评估上，把"仅语义拓扑"接口与"还需关系几何规范"接口在60条指令上对比；在8种关系形式、64次接地评估中检验任务映射是否保留注册零方向、是否响应关系相关扰动，并比较解析方向导数与有限差分、雅可比秩与注册维数；最后在MuJoCo的Franka Emika Panda上做三次闭环消融。

Result: 两种接口在41/60条指令上产出正确的共享语义内容，但在各自接口要求下的关键错误分别为19/60与58/60。接地评估中，任务映射保留了注册零方向并响应关系相关扰动，解析方向导数与有限差分一致，雅可比秩与注册维数相符。闭环消融中，固定一个本应保留的关系坐标使终端进度误差中位数增加20.24–71.00 mm，而保留的关系误差仍在其评估界内。

Conclusion: 结果表明，把"关系可见的几何"与"需保留的自由度"一起编译为可组合的连续目标，是一种可行且必要的设计，能够在语言模型的定性与控制器的连续性之间建立清晰的接口分工。

Abstract: Natural-language manipulation instructions specify qualitative relations, whereas continuous controllers require state-evaluable task quantities, differentials, and completion conditions. Because a qualitative relation generally leaves part of the relative configuration unspecified, expanding it into a complete pose can introduce unintended constraints. We present a typed semantic-to-geometric interface in which language specifies entities, relations, and phases, while each relation indexes a registered specification of its task-relevant distinctions and preserved freedoms. A robot-side compiler grounds these specifications, constructs relation-specific task maps and consistent differentials using conformal geometric algebra, and composes the resulting policies through RMPflow. To evaluate the division of responsibility between the language model and the compiler, we compared a Semantic Topology interface with one that additionally requires relation-specific geometric specifications over 60 instructions. Both produced correct shared semantic content in 41/60 cases, but critical errors under their respective interface requirements occurred in 19/60 and 58/60 cases. Across 64 grounded evaluations spanning eight geometric relation forms, the task maps preserved registered null directions and responded to relation-relevant perturbations; analytic directional derivatives agreed with finite differences, and Jacobian ranks matched the registered dimensions. In three closed-loop ablations using a simulated Franka Emika Panda in MuJoCo, fixing a relation-preserved coordinate increased median terminal progress error by 20.24--71.00~mm while the retained relation errors remained within their evaluation bounds. These results support compiling relation-visible geometry and preserved freedom together into composable continuous objectives.

</details>


### [134] [From World Models to World Action Models: Rethinking Next-State Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34414)
*Tingyu Yuan,Ziming Ji,Biaoliang Guan,Wen Ye,Wenrui Tian,Zhaopeng Gu,Feihong Zhang,Xu Yang,Yan Huang,Zhaowen Li,Chaoyang Zhao,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CF-WAM，一种动态下一状态预测的世界-动作模型框架，通过采样同一未来的多种投影（视觉、语义、几何、交互）并统一为视频形式进行监督，从而突破单一表示的归纳偏置限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型虽转向世界-动作模型，但下一状态在训练前被固定为RGB、单一隐特征或预定义目标的静态组合，使动作学习受限于特定表示的归纳偏置，也阻碍跨具身（人类与机器人）经验的共享。

Method: 动态采样同一未来状态的视觉、语义、几何与交互投影，标准化为统一视频形式，在训练中联合监督一个统一的WAM；这些投影暴露的动作相关约束随训练步骤累积，迫使模型学习支撑同一动作条件未来多种投影的状态转移结构，并为人类与机器人学习提供自然的跨具身动力学参考系。

Result: CF-WAM提升训练效率与最终控制性能，有效将人类经验转化为策略增益；在RoboCasa-GR1上平均成功率82.50%，LIBERO-Plus上82.65%，真实世界最高达84.00%，达到最新最好水平。

Conclusion: 动态多投影的下一状态预测机制能突破单一表示的归纳偏置，促进跨具身状态转移学习，是一种兼顾效率与性能且可迁移人类经验的WAM范式。

Abstract: Predicting the next state is a core paradigm of World Models for modeling physical dynamics, emphasizing prediction fidelity. As World Models evolve into World-Action Models (WAMs), existing methods still fix the next state before training as RGB, a single latent feature, or a static combination of predefined targets, thereby constraining action learning to the inductive biases preserved by a particular representation. To address this limitation, we propose CF-WAM, a dynamic next-state prediction framework that samples visual, semantic, geometric, and interaction projections of the same future, standardizes them into a common video form, and supervises a unified WAM across these projections. The action-relevant constraints exposed by these projections accumulate across training steps, forcing WAM to capture the underlying state-transition structure that supports multiple projections of the same action-conditioned future. This dynamic mechanism also provides a natural cross-embodiment dynamics reference frame for Human and Robot learning. By jointly learning across different next-state parameterizations, heterogeneous Human and Robot experience can bypass appearance differences and directly contribute to shared state-transition learning, improving cross-embodiment generalization. Experiments show that CF-WAM improves both training efficiency and final control performance, while translating Human experience effectively into policy gains. CF-WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on RoboCasa-GR1 with an average success rate of 82.50%, while reaching 82.65% on LIBERO-Plus and up to 84.00% in real-world evaluations.

</details>


### [135] [ARS: Agentic Reward System for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34484)
*Sheng Hu,Weiyi Lu,Lingbing Zeng,Gan Weng,Weiwei Zhang,Kai Xie,Xiaofeng Mou,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Reward System（ARS），一种无需额外奖励模型训练的推理框架，利用通用VLM进行进度奖励建模，通过子代理提议事件时间线、主代理验证修正，提升机器人奖励估计可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进度奖励建模需区分有意义的状态变化与失败尝试及任务无关动作。现有奖励模型即使在简单pick-and-place场景中，也会对错误物体操作赋予虚假进度，可靠性不足。

Method: ARS基于通用VLM，无需额外奖励模型训练。给定离线轨迹和任务指令，采用自适应视觉检查进行事件提议与验证：子代理提出任务相关事件时间线，主代理验证并修正，再估计逐帧进度。可选引入终端结果标签和视觉参考，并可审计外部奖励模型的进度估计。以27B VLM进行评估。

Result: 在受控语义失配基准上，多个奖励基线在简单场景中对错误物体操作赋予虚假进度，ARS更好地抑制此类错误；在仿真策略学习中优于这些基线。并在真实机器人多螺钉紧固任务（工业洗衣机装配线全尺寸实验室复制环境）上，支持从混合质量离线经验中进行长时程策略学习。

Conclusion: 结构化推理与验证可提升通用VLM在机器人奖励建模中的实用性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Progress reward modeling is the problem of estimating how a robot's behavior changes task progress over time. Reliable estimation requires distinguishing meaningful state changes from failed attempts and task-irrelevant actions. We introduce the Agentic Reward System (ARS), an inference framework for progress reward modeling with general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs), without additional reward-model training. Given an offline trajectory and a task instruction, ARS uses adaptive visual inspection for both event proposal and verification. A subagent proposes a task-relevant event timeline, which a primary agent verifies and revises before estimating per-frame progress. ARS can incorporate optional terminal outcome labels and visual references to inform its judgments. It can also audit progress estimates from external reward models. We evaluate ARS with a 27B VLM on a controlled semantic-mismatch benchmark and downstream policy learning in simulation and on a real robot. The benchmark reveals that several evaluated reward baselines assign spurious progress to wrong-object manipulation even in simple pick-and-place scenes. ARS better suppresses these errors and outperforms these baselines in simulation policy learning. We further demonstrate that ARS supports long-horizon policy learning from mixed-quality offline experience on real-robot multi-screw fastening in a full-scale laboratory replica of an industrial washing-machine assembly line. These results suggest that structured inference and verification can improve the usefulness of general-purpose VLMs for robot reward modeling. Code is at https://github.com/midea-ai/ars

</details>


### [136] [Trajectory-Safe Orienteering for Human-Robot Shared Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34485)
*Songqun Gao,Elena Basei,Marco Roveri,Luigi Palopoli,Daniele Fontanelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究带时间窗和可变收益的定向问题（OPTWVP），提出两阶段解耦离散-连续优化方法 DeCoST，在共享空间中生成无碰撞且高质量的轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在共享工作空间中同时保证轨迹安全可行与任务执行质量，因此需要研究 OPTWVP 问题。

Method: 提出两阶段解耦离散-连续优化结合服务时间引导轨迹（DeCoST）方法；引入节点的安全感知时间窗与离散化工作空间，以确保末端执行器与人类之间无碰撞轨迹。

Result: 初步结果验证了 DeCoST 在生成无碰撞轨迹规划的同时保持定向任务质量的有效性。

Conclusion: DeCoST 能有效求解共享空间中的 OPTWVP，兼顾安全性与任务执行质量。

Abstract: Orienteering problem (OP) has wide real-world applications and also great potential in human-robot collaboration. However, existing approaches struggle to simultaneously ensure safe and feasible trajectories while achieving high-quality task execution in shared workspaces. To this end, this work studies the OP with time windows and variable profits (OPTWVP). A two-stage DEcoupled discrete-Continuous Optimization with Service-time-guided Trajectory (DeCoST) approach is proposed to effectively solve OPTWVP in shared spaces. Meanwhile, the safety-aware time windows of nodes and the discretized workspace are introduced to ensure collision-free trajectories between the end effector and the human. Preliminary results validate the effectiveness of DeCoST in generating collision-free trajectory plans while preserving the quality of orienteering tasks.

</details>


### [137] [Model-Informed Safe Reinforcement Learning for Bipedal Locomotion via Step-to-Step Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34486)
*Victor Paredes,Ayonga Hereid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型启发的强化学习框架，以ALIP模板为锚点，通过离散指数控制屏障函数（DECBF）为步态提供安全证书，并将其用于训练塑形与运行时动作过滤，在Digit人形机器人上验证了安全违规事件的减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在杂乱、以人为中心的环境中具备通用移动潜力，但实际部署需要原则性的安全保障。传统基于模型的步态生成器可解释但鲁棒性和适应性不足，而现代强化学习方法虽鲁棒适应性强却缺乏安全保证。

Method: 提出模型启发的强化学习框架，锚定于解析的角动量线性倒立摆（ALIP）模板。通过离散指数控制屏障函数（DECBF）为ALIP步态提供逐步安全证书，并将其作为（i）训练时的塑形信号和（ii）运行时动作过滤器，最小化调整摆动足落点以满足模板级约束。在MuJoCo中对Digit人形机器人配合全身控制器栈进行全阶安全性的实证评估。

Result: 与无约束基线相比，该方法在报告的外部扰动试验中减少了安全违规事件；但在较大的横向速度瞬变下暴露出安全与跟踪之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 模型启发的强化学习结合ALIP模板与DECBF安全证书，可在保持学习策略适应性的同时提升人形机器人步态安全，但安全性与速度跟踪性能之间存在权衡，需进一步平衡。

Abstract: Humanoid robots promise versatile mobility in cluttered, human-centric environments, but real deployment demands principled safety. Classical model-based gait generators yield interpretable motions but often lack the robustness and adaptability of modern reinforcement learning (RL) based approaches. We propose a model-informed reinforcement learning framework anchored to the analytical Angular Momentum Linear Inverted Pendulum (ALIP) template. We provide a step-to-step safety certificate for ALIP stepping via a discrete exponential control barrier function (DECBF) and use it as (i) a training-time shaping signal and (ii) a runtime action filter that minimally adjusts swing-foot placement to satisfy template-level constraints. Full-order safety is evaluated empirically on the Digit humanoid in MuJoCo with a whole-body controller stack. Compared to an unconstrained baseline, our approach reduces safety-violation events in the reported external-disturbance trial, while larger lateral-velocity transients reveal a safety-tracking tradeoff.

</details>


### [138] [Robot-Assisted Deployment and Maintenance of Inflatable Modules for Lunar Habitation: A Field Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34498)
*Kentaro Uno,Shamistan Karimov,Ashutosh Mishra,Elian Neppel,Hazal Gozbasi,Shreya Santra,Shinichi Kimura,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出由充气模块与模块化可重构机器人组成的月球居住系统，并通过真实硬件演示验证机器人辅助部署与维护任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球长期人类居住与原位开发需要机器人技术支持未来人类前哨的建设，需要提升居住模块部署与维护的可靠性与自动化水平。

Method: 构建模块化可重构机器人 MoonBot 与充气模块 HIDAS 的组合系统，开展真实硬件任务演示，包括充气过程机器人巡检、模块位置对齐、最终安全锁定及部署后维护三维建图。

Result: 各项任务演示均在实验室环境或月球模拟试验场成功完成，验证了系统在部署与维护中的可行性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 总结了经验教训，为机器人应用于未来月球居住提供关键见解，并为后续研究与应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Long-term human habitation and in-situ development on the Moon open a new era of space utilization. In this context, robots are a key technology for facilitating the construction of future human outposts. Toward the deployment and establishment of human habitation modules on the lunar surface, we propose a combined system consisting of inflatable modules and a modular, reconfigurable robotic system. This paper presents a report demonstrating various robot-assisted task executions using real hardware, namely the modular and reconfigurable robot MoonBot and the inflatable module HIDAS, to enhance the reliability of their deployment and maintenance. The demonstrated tasks include robotic inspection during inflation, module position alignment, final safety locking, and three-dimensional mapping for post-deployment maintenance. All demonstrations were conducted either in a laboratory environment or at a lunar analogue test site. Finally, lessons learned are discussed to provide essential insights for this robotic application to future lunar habitation.

</details>


### [139] [MonoEgo: Monocular Metric Egocentric Demonstration Capture with Passive Wrist Constellations and Sparse Workstation Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34512)
*Jie Xu,Kangjin Yu,Ziyi Jin,Beichen Wang,Zhongpu Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoEgo 提出一种仅用单目全局快门相机的离线捕捉系统，用被动腕部标记星座替代主动腕部追踪硬件，通过 MonoTag SLAM 实现度量级轨迹重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像对齐的度量演示通常依赖专用追踪硬件以及多设备间的同步，部署成本高、限制大，作者希望降低捕捉端的要求。

Method: 采用一台 90-FPS 全局快门相机，在同一图像时钟下观测标定过的被动腕部星座、稀疏工作台锚点和场景；MonoTag SLAM 将标记角点与 ORB 几何结合，并利用视觉证据剔除歧义的平面标记位姿；度量 Atlas 支持区间尺度重锚定、经验证的地图合并以及对早期帧的回顾式定位。

Result: 实验显示系统能在锚点连续可见范围之外进行度量追踪、重连受支持的地图组件，并恢复部分缺失的相机位姿；与多传感器相机参考及独立静止星座测试的对比刻画了轨迹一致性与精度，同时暴露了覆盖不完整和残余几何不确定性。

Conclusion: 结果表明被动夹具与离线重建可降低捕捉端需求，但动态精度、实际部署及下游策略收益仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Image-aligned metric demonstrations often require dedicated tracking hardware and synchronization across devices. We present MonoEgo, a capture system that replaces active wrist instrumentation with offline monocular reconstruction. One 90-FPS global-shutter camera observes calibrated passive wrist constellations, sparse workstation anchors, and the scene on a shared image clock. MonoTag SLAM combines marker corners with ORB geometry and uses visual evidence to reject ambiguous planar-marker poses. Its metric Atlas supports interval scale re-anchoring, verified map merging, and retrospective localization of earlier frames supported by the final map. Camera and wrist-constellation outputs retain validity and map provenance, and unsupported motion is left missing. Experiments show metric tracking beyond continuous anchor visibility, reconnection of supported map components, and recovery of some missing camera poses. Comparisons against a multisensor camera reference and separate stationary-constellation tests characterize trajectory agreement and precision while revealing incomplete coverage and residual geometric uncertainty. The results indicate that passive fixtures and offline reconstruction can reduce capture-side requirements. Dynamic accuracy, deployment, and downstream policy benefits require further study.

</details>


### [140] [Gaze Prompts: Temporally Dense Human Attention for Vision-Language-Action Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34550)
*Yihan Zhou,Rui Yan,Mingcong Li,Zheyuan Huang,Xu Yang,Xueyang Guo,Yilin Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"眼动追踪监督的注视提示"方法，利用VR遥操作中记录的眼动为VLA微调提供帧级视觉引导，部署时用轻量预测器推断注视位置，无需眼动仪即可提升操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA微调在每步配对图像与动作，但通常只提供任务级语言指令，导致逐帧的视觉相关性是隐式的，模型缺少"当下该看哪里"的引导。

Method: 训练阶段将VR遥操作记录的眼动位置以十字准星渲染到机器人头部相机图像上；部署阶段用轻量预测器根据近期图像与指令估计注视点，生成同样形式的视觉提示，无需眼动仪或修改策略架构。基于π₀实例化。

Result: 在六个真实世界双臂操作任务上，平均成功率从26.3%提升到56.0%；单策略训练全部六任务时同样有增益。同时发布GazeMani数据集，含1200条同步眼动的遥操作轨迹。

Conclusion: 帧级注视提示能有效增强VLA的视觉引导，且可通过预测器在部署时低成本复现，并贡献了带眼动标注的数据集。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) fine-tuning pairs images with actions at every step, yet typically provides only a task-level language instruction, leaving moment-to-moment visual relevance implicit. We introduce \emph{eye-tracker-supervised gaze prompting}, which uses gaze recorded during VR teleoperation to provide frame-level visual guidance for VLA fine-tuning. During training, recorded gaze locations are rendered as crosshairs on the robot's head-camera images. At deployment, a lightweight predictor estimates gaze locations from recent images and the instruction, supplying the same type of visual prompt without an eye tracker or changes to the policy architecture. Instantiated with $π_0$, gaze prompting increases mean success from $26.3\%$ to $56.0\%$ across six real-world bimanual manipulation tasks, with gains also observed when a single policy is trained on all six tasks. We release \textsc{GazeMani}, a dataset of $1{,}200$ teleoperated trajectories with synchronized gaze.

</details>


### [141] [Where Memory Belongs: Ledger, an Object Ledger for Memory-Augmented VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34554)
*Tanguy Dieudonné,Jack B. Jedlicki,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出Ledger，将机器人操作中的记忆按类型分工：短期感知记忆放在策略内部，长期物体记忆作为外部可读记录（ledger），在RoboMME基准上取得四套件平均最高64.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程、部分可观测的机器人操作需要记忆能力（如记住物体放进了哪个抽屉、移动了谁的杯子、经过了多少动作周期）。当前VLA模型把记忆直接嵌入策略内部，但基准测试显示没有单一的策略内机制能覆盖所有时空维度，且大幅落后于oracle方法。

Method: 提出Ledger框架，基于单一微调的π_{0.5}策略实现记忆分工：策略内部使用帧采样记忆处理短期感知记忆（重复、计时、回溯）；外部使用由SAM3追踪器和VLM描述器构建的时空物体记忆ledger，记录持久空间状态、容器包含关系和事件历史，并由LLM规划器在步骤边界读取决策。运行时根据指令和记录选择记忆来源，无需任务级路由。

Result: 在RoboMME上，Ledger在评估方法中取得最高四套件平均64.3%（最强先前方法为45.9%），物体指代60.7%（对比40.3%），物体持久性86.7%（对比56.2%），且使用单一权重集。

Conclusion: 记忆类型决定其存放位置：短期感知记忆适合策略内部，长期物体记忆适合作为外部显式记录。这种划分方式在无需任务级路由器的情况下有效提升了机器人操作中的记忆性能。

Abstract: Memory is essential for long-horizon, partially observed robotic manipulation: a robot must remember which object was placed in a drawer, whose cup it moved, or how many action cycles have elapsed. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models embed memory directly inside the policy, but benchmarks show no single in-policy mechanism covers all spatio-temporal dimensions, trailing oracle methods by a wide margin. We argue that memory type dictates where memory should reside: short-term perceptual memory (repetition, timing, retracing) belongs inside the policy, while long-term object memory (persistent spatial state, containment, event history) belongs outside as an explicit, readable record. We present Ledger, a harness that realizes this split over a single fine-tuned $π_{0.5}$ policy by pairing an in-policy frame-sampling memory with an external spatio-temporal object memory, the ledger, built from a SAM3 tracker and a VLM captioner of the demonstration and read by an LLM planner that decides at step boundaries. On RoboMME, Ledger reaches the highest four-suite average among the evaluated methods, 64.3% (vs. 45.9% for the strongest prior method under identical evaluation), leading object reference (60.7% vs. 40.3%) and object permanence (86.7% vs. 56.2%) using a single set of weights. Choosing the memory source at runtime, from the instruction and the record, removes the need for a task-level router.

</details>


### [142] [Aerial GRIPPER: A Gradient-based Real-time Inverse-game Predictor and Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34594)
*Zeshuai Chen,Meng Wang,Jindou Jia,Xiang Yu,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GRIPPER框架，用梯度实时逆博弈预测与规划实现非合作目标的高频捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作目标的精确捕获是极具挑战性的难题，目标成本参数未知且需实时对抗决策。

Method: 将交互建模为不完全信息下的一般和追逃博弈，在线推断目标成本参数，在滚动时域内迭代求解开环纳什均衡；开发基于梯度的逆博弈求解器，利用隐式微分与快速Hessian向量积避免Hessian逆运算，实现>50 Hz更新；并设计抗扰动控制器应对载荷与夹爪执行扰动。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明GRIPPER在计算效率和任务性能上更优，成功完成非合作目标的捕获与递送。

Conclusion: 该框架在高度对抗场景中表现出鲁棒性、适应性和实时性。

Abstract: Accurate capture of non-cooperative targets is critical. In an attempt to tackle this intractable challenge, an aerial gripper system integrated with a Gradient-based Real-time Inverse-game Predictor and PlannER (GRIPPER) framework is proposed. The interaction is formulated as a general-sum pursuit-evasion game under incomplete information. Specifically, underlying cost parameters of the target are inferred online, and the open-loop Nash equilibrium (OLNE) strategy is iteratively refined within a receding-horizon loop. To ensure high-frequency execution, a computationally friendly gradient-based inverse-game solver is developed.
