Without explicit computation of the Hessian inverse, the optimized solution is updated (> 50 Hz) based on implicit differentiation and fast Hessian-vector products. Meanwhile, an anti-disturbance controller is developed to overcome disturbances of uncertain payload and gripper actuation, enabling precise tracking of the planned trajectory and accurate grasping of the target. Simulations and real-world experiments illustrate the superior computational efficiency and task performance of GRIPPER. The task of capturing and delivering a non-cooperative target is accomplished, highlighting the robustness, adaptability, and real-time performance of the framework in highly adversarial scenarios.

</details>


### [143] [Efficient World Action Model Inference with Adaptive Intermediate States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34608)
*Zhinnan Liu,Haozhi Han,Ruge Zhang,Teng Ma,Tao Ma,Zheng Liu,Yifeng Chen,Yunquan Zhang,Ting Cao,Yunxin Liu,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架WAMACHINE，通过保留并自适应推理状态加速世界动作模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过联合建模动作与环境动态实现未来感知控制，但迭代扩散或流推理带来大量去噪延迟。先前推理状态提供了加速机会，然而规划上下文、观测和中间表示的变化会迅速使保留状态失效，因此需要“自适应”而非直接复用状态。

Method: WAMACHINE在三个层面自适应推理状态：1）跨闭环重规划，Trajectory Remapping将上一次重规划状态重映射以初始化下一次重规划，减少冗余轨迹生成；2）跨去噪步，Observation Rebinding在执行动作时进行预判推理，并在一致性检查通过时将保留的去噪状态与真实观测重新绑定以延续，降低控制循环暴露的延迟；3）跨Transformer层，Residual Rescaling在探针检查失败时选择性重新缩放保留的层状态，并通过中间层全量计算刷新，减少重复Transformer计算。

Result: 在LIBERO与RoboTwin 2.0上对三种代表性WAM架构评估：观测到动作延迟加速1.47-3.05倍，每次重规划GPU推理时间加速2.23-3.27倍，同时保留96.69-99.54%的原生WAM任务成功率。

Conclusion: WAMACHINE是一种免训练框架，通过保留与自适应推理状态，在保持任务成功率的同时显著降低WAM推理延迟，实现高效准确的控制延续。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable future-aware control by jointly modeling actions and environment dynamics. However, iterative diffusion or flow inference incurs substantial denoising latency. Prior inference state offers a natural opportunity for acceleration, yet changing planning contexts, observations, and intermediate representations can quickly render retained state stale. Preserving useful computation therefore requires adapting inference state rather than reusing it as-is. To this end, we present $\mathrm{WAM}{\scriptstyle\mathrm{ACHINE}}$, a training-free framework that accelerates WAM inference by preserving and adapting inference state for efficient and accurate continuation as the control loop evolves. Across closed-loop replans, Trajectory Remapping remaps replan state from the preceding replan to initialize the next replan, reducing redundant trajectory generation. Across denoising steps, Observation Rebinding performs anticipatory inference during action execution and rebinds retained denoising state to the real observation for continuation when consistency checks pass, reducing latency exposed to the control loop. Across Transformer layers, Residual Rescaling selectively rescales retained layer state and refreshes it through full computation of the middle layers when probe checks fail, reducing repeated Transformer computation. Evaluations of three representative WAM architectures on LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0 show that $\mathrm{WAM}{\scriptstyle\mathrm{ACHINE}}$ achieves 1.47-3.05$\times$ speedups in observation-to-action latency and 2.23-3.27$\times$ speedups in GPU inference time per replan, while preserving 96.69-99.54% of native WAM task success.

</details>


### [144] [On the Numerical Reliability of Differentiable Physics-Based Optimization for Robotic Material Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34666)
*Xintong Yang,Minglun Wei,Yu-Kun Lai,Ze Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了可微物理仿真在机器人材料操作中长时程、接触密集滚动梯度的数值可靠性。通过两个MPM系统辨识基准（弹塑性与颗粒操作），揭示了三类影响优化结果的问题：GPU非确定性求和改变长时程梯度甚至反转梯度符号；有限差分校验在长滚动中因重复运行波动增长过快而失效；观测与损失定义的选择会改变优化行为及独立指标偏好的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微物理被广泛用于机器人材料操作，包括系统辨识、轨迹/技能优化、示范生成以及机器人或末端执行器设计，这些应用都依赖于通过长时程、接触密集仿真滚动传播的梯度。然而这类梯度的数值可靠性尚不明确，作者希望系统性地研究其可靠性问题。

Method: 作者构建了两个源自弹塑性与颗粒操作的MPM系统辨识基准，为三类效应提供受控测试用例：分析GPU many-to-one求和的线程调度顺序对长时程梯度的影响（与确定性参考对比）；通过有限差分检验比较扰动引起的损失变化与重复运行波动；考察不同观测与损失定义对优化行为的影响。

Result: 依赖线程调度的GPU many-to-one求和改变了长时程梯度，并使某一参数梯度相对确定性参考发生符号反转；随着滚动变长，重复运行损失波动增长远快于参数扰动带来的损失变化，导致有限差分检验可靠性下降；观测与损失定义改变优化行为及独立指标所偏好的解。

Conclusion: 这些结果提示在将可微仿真用于机器人优化时，应采用可复现的累加方式、将扰动引起的损失变化与重复运行波动进行对比的有限差分验证，并显式报告目标函数的构建方式。

Abstract: Differentiable physics is increasingly used in robotic material manipulation for system identification, trajectory or skill optimization, demonstration generation, and robot or end-effector design. These applications depend on gradients propagated through long, contact-rich simulation rollouts. We study the numerical reliability of those gradients using two Material Point Method (MPM) system-identification benchmarks derived from elastoplastic and granular manipulation. The benchmarks provide controlled cases for three effects that also arise in broader differentiable physics-based optimization. GPU many-to-one sums whose order depends on thread scheduling changed long-horizon gradients and reversed the sign of one parameter gradient relative to a deterministic reference. Finite-difference checks became less reliable for longer rollouts because repeated-run loss variation grew much faster than the loss change produced by the tested parameter perturbations. Observation and loss definitions changed optimization behaviour and the solution preferred by an independent metric. These results motivate reproducible accumulation, finite-difference validation that compares perturbation-induced loss changes with repeated-run variation, and explicit reporting of objective construction when differentiable simulation is used for robotic optimization.

</details>


### [145] [HOI-Retarget: Contact-Centric Retargeting for Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34674)
*Jihwan Shin,Adrià López Escoriza,Junzhe He,Matthias Heyrman,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOI-Retarget 是一种以接触为中心的重定向方法，将人-物交互（HOI）动作迁移到人形机器人上，用于大规模运动数据生成，并公开代码与数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人虽能通过示教学习（LfD）获得多样的全身技能，但将该范式扩展到人-物交互时，受限于缺少与机器人兼容的交互参考数据。

Method: 采用基于窗口的轨迹优化，以每个带标注的接触点在物体坐标系中作为目标，在机器人运动学限制下平衡身体跟踪、足部支撑与平滑性；可将单一示教扩展到不同物体尺寸，融合单目视频重建的接触信息，并支持多机器人操作同一物体。

Result: 方法能够跨物体尺寸增强单次示教、吸收单目视频重建的接触，并扩展到多机器人协作操作同一物体；同时公开了代码与重定向运动数据集。

Conclusion: HOI-Retarget 为将人-物交互技能迁移到人形机器人提供了可行方案，并支持大规模运动数据生成。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) has enabled humanoid robots to acquire diverse whole-body skills, but extending this paradigm to human-object interaction (HOI) is limited by the availability of robot-compatible interaction references. We present HOI-Retarget, a contact-centric retargeting method that transfers HOI onto a humanoid robot for large-scale motion-data generation. Its windowed trajectory optimization uses every labeled contact as a target in the object frame, balancing body tracking, foot support and smoothness under the robot's kinematic limits. The method can augment a single demonstration across object sizes, absorb contacts reconstructed from monocular video, and extend to several robots manipulating one object. We publicly release the code and the retargeted motion dataset.

</details>


### [146] [Natural State-Prediction Accuracy can Hide Weak Controlled Responsiveness in VLA Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34684)
*Hyungjoon Kim,Wonbin Son,Mi Young Lee,Jun Young Lee,Seungmin Rho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估框架，将预测准确率、目标状态响应性与上下文稳定性分离，揭示高自然轨迹准确率可能伴随弱的目标状态响应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的内部表征中准确解码物体状态，并不能证明预测能忠实响应目标物理状态的变化。自然观测中物体状态、机器人构型、遮挡与任务进度共同变化，上下文线索可能混入预测，因此需要区分准确率与真正的状态响应性。

Method: 提出一个评估框架，使用经过物理验证的观测数据，将目标坐标与机器人上下文交叉组合，从而分离三个属性：预测准确率、目标状态响应性、上下文稳定性。并对表征、读出模块和训练数据进行对比与干预实验。

Result: 在固定的表征-读出组合中，高的自然轨迹准确率可以与受控条件下弱的目标状态响应性共存。三类属性提供不同的诊断信息。在基于初始状态误差和物理变量的失败预测器中加入响应性与上下文敏感性，可在新初始化条件下降低策略失败预测误差，同时同观测受控MAE也具有信息量。

Conclusion: 应结合自然预测准确率一同评估目标状态响应性与上下文稳定性，并进一步考察它们与实际策略行为和任务结果之间的关系。

Abstract: Accurately decoding object states from the internal representations of vision-language-action (VLA) models does not establish that the predictions respond faithfully to changes in the target physical state. In natural observations, object state, robot configuration, occlusion, and task progress vary together, allowing contextual cues to contribute to prediction. In this paper, we introduce an evaluation framework that separates prediction accuracy, target-state responsiveness, and context stability using physically validated observations that cross target coordinates with robot contexts. We demonstrate that high natural-trajectory accuracy can coexist with weak controlled target-state responsiveness in fixed representation-readout pairs. Comparisons and interventions involving representations, readouts, and training data show that the three properties provide distinct diagnostic information. Furthermore, adding responsiveness and context sensitivity to a failure predictor based on initial state error and physical variables reduces policy-failure prediction error on new initializations relative to the specified baseline while same-observation controlled MAE is also informative. These findings motivate evaluating target-state responsiveness and context stability alongside natural prediction accuracy, and examining their relationship to actual policy behavior and task outcomes.

</details>


### [147] [Sufficiency of Zeroth-Order Reward Shaping for Policy Gradient in Stabilization Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34695)
*Yisheng Zhang,Tao Wang,Sicun Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人强化学习中的奖励塑形，指出实践中常混用控制目标内在项与数值正则项，导致超参数脆弱。作者以镇定控制为对象，从理论与实验证明：策略梯度可在无速度（一阶）奖励项时求解镇定任务，加入一阶奖励项反而随尺度增大带来敏感性；而零阶（构型）奖励项必须在目标相关坐标上完备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励塑形方法多沿用经典最优控制与轨迹优化的启发式原则，很少区分哪些奖励项是控制目标的内在要求、哪些只是数值正则项，因此超参数调节脆弱。作者希望确定奖励函数中"必须"包含哪些量。

Method: 聚焦镇定控制问题，区分零阶（构型/位置）与一阶（速度）信息；结合理论分析与经验实验，考察策略梯度方法在不同奖励构成下的表现，并测试一阶奖励项尺度变化带来的敏感性。

Result: 理论与实验均表明：策略梯度方法无需一阶奖励项即可成功完成镇定任务；加入一阶奖励项会随其尺度增大引入严重敏感性。同时，奖励函数必须在目标相关坐标上具备零阶完备性，而在低耗散假设下，一阶状态信息仍需保留在策略观测中。

Conclusion: 研究为机器人强化学习的奖励设计提供了可操作且有原则的指导：奖励应保证零阶完备、避免不必要的一阶奖励项，同时将速度等一阶状态纳入策略观测。

Abstract: Reward shaping is fundamental to modern robotic control with deep reinforcement learning (RL), yet practitioners still rely heavily on heuristic principles borrowed from classical optimal control and trajectory optimization. Existing methods rarely distinguish reward terms that are intrinsic to the control objective from numerical regularizers, leading to brittle hyperparameter tuning. To determine which quantities a reward must contain, we study the stabilization control problem with a focus on zeroth-order (configuration) and first-order (velocity) information. We theoretically and empirically demonstrate that policy gradient methods can successfully solve stabilization tasks without first-order reward terms, adding such terms can instead introduce severe sensitivity as their scale grows. Conversely, our findings confirm that reward functions must be zeroth-order complete over goal-relevant coordinates, while the first-order state remains necessary in the policy observation under our low-dissipation assumptions. Overall, these results provide actionable and principled guidance for reward design in robotic RL.

</details>


### [148] [MarsLab: A Martian Rover Simulator for Planetary Rover Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34702)
*Hoyun Kim,Beomsu Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MarsLab 是一个开源、ROS2 原生的火星车仿真器，结合 HiRISE 衍生与程序化地形，在 Isaac Sim 中运行 Perseverance 级火星车，用于自主导航算法开发并可开展 SLAM 与 VPR 基准评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来火星任务需要火星车在非结构化地形、光照变化、大气尘埃和通信受限条件下自主运行。现有火星相关仿真资源范围各异：任务导向仿真器、固定模拟数据集、任务专用环境以及开放机器人接口，缺乏统一且可控的评测平台。

Method: 构建 MarsLab：开源、ROS2 原生；融合 HiRISE 衍生地形与程序化地形；提供可定制的岩石、陨石坑、太阳光照与大气尘埃设置；在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 中运行 Perseverance 级火星车模型；运行时通过标准 ROS2 话题发布 RGB、深度、RGB-D 点云、LiDAR、IMU、轮式里程计与真值位姿数据。

Result: 展示了 SLAM 基准评测，覆盖多种传感模态、尘埃水平、场景几何与路径长度；并展示了在光照与尘埃变化下对 Mars Base 多次遍历的 VPR 基准评测。结果表明受控场景变化与共享真值轨迹可在同一仿真器内比较轨迹级估计与图像级场景识别。

Conclusion: MarsLab 提供了统一可控的火星环境仿真与数据接口，支持在共享真值条件下比较 SLAM 与 VPR 方法，为火星自主导航算法开发与评测提供实用平台。项目主页：https://kimhoyun-robotair.github.io/MarsLab/

Abstract: Future Mars missions will require rover autonomy that can operate across unstructured terrain, changing illumination, atmospheric dust, and limited communication. Simulation is a practical way to study these conditions before deployment, but existing Mars-relevant resources differ in scope, including mission-oriented simulators, fixed analog datasets, task-specific environments, and open robotics interfaces. In this context, we present MarsLab, an open-source, ROS2-native Mars rover simulator for autonomy and navigation algorithm development. MarsLab combines HiRISE-derived and procedural terrain with customizable rock, crater, solar-illumination, and atmospheric-dust settings, and runs a Perseverance-class rover model in NVIDIA Isaac Sim. The runtime publishes RGB, depth, RGB-D point clouds, LiDAR, IMU, wheel odometry, and Ground Truth (GT) pose data through standard ROS2 topics. We demonstrate MarsLab with Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) benchmarks across sensing modalities, dust levels, scene geometry, and route length, and with Visual Place Recognition (VPR) benchmarks over repeated Mars Base traversals under illumination and dust changes. The results illustrate how controlled scene variation and shared GT trajectories can be used to compare trajectory-level estimation and image-level place recognition within the same simulator. Our Project Page: https://kimhoyun-robotair.github.io/MarsLab/.

</details>


### [149] [SOR-Nav: Search or Relocate? Context-Gated Exploration and Cross-Region Relocation for Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34707)
*Yuan Ji,Zirui Li,Yuxin Cai,Shuge Wu,Boon Siew Han,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOR-Nav是一种分层目标导航系统，通过在当前上下文继续探索与跨区域转移之间进行显式仲裁，在HM3D-v1/v2和MP3D基准上取得最优成功率与SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标导航方法主要通过在候选目的地排序来优化下一步位置，缺乏对"继续探索当前区域"还是"放弃当前区域转向更有希望的可达区域"的显式决策能力，导致在部分可观测和有限移动预算下搜索效率受限。

Method: 构建自主语义探索系统，累积持久3D物体簇并将可达前沿组织为簇决策图，提供高效搜索抽象；再用上下文门控的LLM驱动物体搜索监督器评估当前搜索上下文适宜性，决定继续探索或跨区域迁移至另一可达前沿簇。

Result: 在HM3D-v1、HM3D-v2和MP3D完整未过滤验证集上取得报告最优SR与SPL；MP3D上SPL从18.1%提升至38.5%（翻倍以上），SR从50.7%提升至61.8%；嵌套HM3D-v2消融验证决策结构有效性，三目标连续物理部署验证真实场景持续运行。

Conclusion: 显式的探索-迁移仲裁决策结构可显著提升目标导航的效率与成功率，且能在真实环境中持续运行。

Abstract: Object navigation requires an embodied agent to find an object in an unseen environment under partial observability and a limited motion budget. Existing methods primarily optimize where the robot should go next by ranking candidate destinations. In contrast to these methods, we present SOR-Nav, a hierarchical navigation system that explicitly arbitrates between continuing to explore the current context and abandoning it for a more promising reachable region. First, an autonomous semantic exploration system is built that accumulates persistent 3D object clusters and organizes reachable frontiers into a cluster decision graph to provide an efficient search abstraction. Then, SOR-Nav uses a context-gated LLM-driven object-search supervisor to evaluate the suitability of the current search context and decide whether to continue exploration or perform cross-region relocation to another reachable frontier cluster. Across the complete, unfiltered validation sets of HM3D-v1, HM3D-v2, and MP3D, SOR-Nav achieves the strongest reported Success Rate (SR) and Success weighted by Path Length (SPL) on all three benchmarks. On MP3D in particular, it more than doubles the previous best SPL from 18.1\% to 38.5\% while increasing SR from 50.7\% to 61.8\%. Nested HM3D-v2 ablations validate the proposed decision structure, while a continuous three-target physical deployment demonstrates persistent ObjectNav operation in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [150] [ExcavaTwin: Training-Free Geometry-Guided Semantic Elevation Mapping for Autonomous Excavation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34719)
*Yu Deng,Lingshan Zeng,Tong Hu,Rushi Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ExcavaTwin，一种纯视觉、几何引导的语义高程建图框架，无需挖掘专用训练，实现地形几何与任务相关语义的联合感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主挖掘需要同时捕捉地形几何与任务相关语义的空间表示，但现有挖掘建图多侧重高程，通用语义模型在非结构化室外场景中不稳定。

Method: 基于多视角 RGB 图像，利用冻结视觉模型重建几何与语义观测，推导地形与非地形几何支撑，进行几何约束的多视角语义融合以抑制不合理预测并补全观测，最终投影为任务导向的语义高程图。

Result: 在公开数据集与真实挖掘场景中实现可靠几何与语义感知；真实挖掘中平均更新间隔约 1.4 s，动态修改区域平均高程误差 12.74 cm，较大误差出现在地形快速变化和机器运动引起的瞬态视觉干扰时。

Conclusion: ExcavaTwin 以纯视觉、免专用训练方式有效结合几何与语义，为自主挖掘提供可用的语义高程建图方案，但在快速地形变化与视觉扰动下仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Autonomous excavation requires a spatial representation that jointly captures terrain geometry and task-relevant semantics. Existing excavation mapping is largely elevation-centric, while generic semantic models remain unstable in unstructured outdoor scenes. We present ExcavaTwin, a pure-vision geometry-guided semantic elevation mapping framework without excavation-specific training. Given multi-view RGB images, the framework: 1) reconstructs scene geometry and semantic observations using frozen vision models; 2) derives terrain and non-terrain geometric support; 3) performs geometry-constrained multi-view semantic fusion to suppress implausible predictions and recover incomplete observations; and 4) projects the fused state into a task-oriented semantic elevation map. Experiments on public datasets and real excavation scenes demonstrate reliable geometric and semantic perception. In real excavation, the system achieved an average update interval of approximately 1.4 s and a mean elevation error of 12.74cm in dynamically modified regions. Larger errors mainly occur during rapid terrain changes and transient visual disturbances caused by machine motion.

</details>


### [151] [DexWeave: Learning Dexterous Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34724)
*Naichuan Sun,Haotian Shen,Yizhang Zhang,Luying Feng,Haoze Wang,Yuanbo Xiangli,Yaochu Jin,Peidong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexWeave 提出统一框架，将交互一致的运动重定向与解剖感知的全身策略学习相结合，实现从人类示范学习灵巧人形 loco-manipulation。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类示范学习灵巧人形 loco-manipulation 时，不仅要迁移人体运动，还要迁移协调的交互结构。由于具身差异，身体运动、手腕位置、手指关节与物体交互之间的耦合会被扭曲，且运动学精确的参考在机器人动力学下仍难实现。

Method: DexWeave 包含两部分：1）两阶段重定向——先用专用求解器初始化身体与手部运动，再在上肢交互链上进行耦合精修，同时保留下肢支撑；2）解剖感知 Transformer 策略——将解剖区域表示为结构化 token，用有向掩码注意力建模依赖，并让物体信息选择性调节上肢通路。策略联合输出身体与灵巧手动作，直接用强化学习训练，无需预训练跟踪策略、师生蒸馏或后续残差精修。

Result: 相比 MLP 基线，DexWeave 提升了重定向保真度与交互一致性，获得更高的操作性能和更快的策略收敛。并在配备 Inspire 灵巧手的 Unitree G1 实体人形上部署，实现真实世界的灵巧全身 loco-manipulation。

Conclusion: DexWeave 通过交互一致重定向与解剖感知全身策略，打通从人类示范到真实人形灵巧全身 loco-manipulation 的路径，且训练流程简洁高效。

Abstract: Learning dexterous humanoid loco-manipulation from human demonstrations requires transferring not only human motion, but also the coordinated interaction structure underlying the demonstrated behavior. This is challenging because embodiment differences distort the coupling among body motion, wrist placement, finger articulation, and object interaction, while kinematically accurate references may still be difficult to realize under robot dynamics. We present DexWeave, a unified framework that connects interaction-consistent motion retargeting with anatomy-aware whole-body policy learning. DexWeave first employs a two-stage retargeting procedure that initializes body and hand motions with specialized solvers and subsequently performs coupled refinement over the upper-body interaction chain while preserving lower-body support. The resulting references are tracked by an anatomy-aware Transformer policy that represents anatomical regions as structured tokens and uses directed masked attention to model their dependencies, with object information selectively conditioning the upper-body pathway for dexterous interaction. The policy jointly outputs body and dexterous-hand actions and is trained directly with reinforcement learning, without pretrained tracking policies, teacher-student distillation, or subsequent residual refinement. DexWeave improves retargeting fidelity and interaction consistency while achieving higher manipulation performance and faster policy convergence than MLP baselines. We further deploy the learned policies on a physical Unitree G1 humanoid equipped with Inspire dexterous hands, demonstrating dexterous whole-body loco-manipulation in the real world. See our project page (https://dexweave.github.io) for videos.

</details>


### [152] [Action Sequence Transfer via LLMs for Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34730)
*Choongho Chung,DongHwan Shin,Sung-Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种动作序列迁移系统，能在不同目标空间之间自适应迁移用户动作序列，并构建基于场景图的新数据集验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户动作序列通常与特定环境的空间布局和物体配置紧密绑定，直接迁移到新环境往往不可行。因此需要一种能适应目标环境空间与物体约束的动作序列迁移方法。

Method: 给定源空间的动作序列以及源、目标环境的场景图表示，系统利用用户活动的多层次表示，在不同抽象层级上对动作进行泛化，从而预测目标空间中对应的动作序列。为评估方法，作者从Ego4D GoalStep数据集构建了基于场景图的新数据集。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统即使在物体配置差异极大的空间之间，也能生成有效的动作序列。

Conclusion: 该方法证明利用多层次动作抽象与场景图约束，可实现跨环境的动作序列迁移，为跨空间的行为泛化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present an action sequence transfer system that adaptively transfers user action sequences across different target spaces. Given an input action sequence from a source space and scene graph representations of both the source and target environments, our system predicts a corresponding action sequence in the target space by adapting to the spatial and object constraints of the new environment. To achieve this, we leverage multi-level representations of user activity to generalize actions at varying levels of abstraction. To demonstrate our system, we collect a new scene graph-based dataset derived from the Ego4D GoalStep dataset for evaluation. Results indicate that our system can generate valid action sequences even between spaces with drastically different object configurations.

</details>


### [153] [Simulation for Planetary Robotic Perception and Autonomy: A Concise Survey of Recent Capabilities and Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34743)
*Hoyun Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了行星机器人感知与自主性的仿真工作，从开放性、场景平台覆盖、传感器支持、环境真实性四个维度展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星机器人科学探索中实地测试昂贵、受限且难以重复，仿真成为感知与自主研究、合成数据生成、系统集成及部署前评估的核心工具，但相关文献分散于不同仿真引擎、实现与应用场景中，缺乏系统梳理。

Method: 对行星机器人感知与自主性的仿真工作进行综述，沿四条实践轴线组织分析：开放性与可获取性、场景与平台覆盖度、传感器与感知支持、环境与运行真实性。

Result: 所综述的仿真工作普遍报告了视觉或物理保真度并支持面向感知的工作流；同时显示公共可获取性不均衡、以巡视器为中心的覆盖、对专用传感模态支持不完整，以及机载计算、能量与通信等运行约束报告不均衡。

Conclusion: 该综述揭示了行星机器人仿真领域的现状与不足，指出开放共享、平台多样性、专用传感器支持及运行约束建模等方面仍有待完善。

Abstract: Planetary robotics is an important enabler of scientific exploration in environments where direct human-in-the-loop operation is costly, hazardous, or infeasible. However, developing and validating planetary robotic systems remains difficult because representative field testing is expensive, limited, and often unrepeatable under mission-relevant conditions. In this setting, simulation serves as a central tool for perception and autonomy research, synthetic data generation, system integration, and pre-deployment evaluation. Despite its importance, the literature on planetary robotics simulation remains dispersed across different simulation engines, implementations, and application settings. This paper surveys simulation works for planetary robotic perception and autonomy across four practical axes: Openness and Availability, Scenario and Platform Coverage, Sensor and Perception Support, and Environmental and Operational Realism. The surveyed simulation works report visual or physical fidelity and support perception-oriented workflows. They also indicate uneven public availability, rover-centered coverage, partial support for specialized sensing modalities, and uneven reporting of operational constraints such as onboard computation, energy, and communication restrictions.

</details>


### [154] [CoHuB: A Simulation Benchmark for Multi-Humanoid Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34782)
*Hyunjin Park,Jebeom Chae,Minwoo Park,Sunghyun Park,Hanjun Yoo,Seoyeon Choi,Soochul Yoo,Joohwan Seo,Sarmad Idrees,Jae-Sang Hyun,Jongmin Lee,Roberto Horowitz,Youngwoon Lee,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoHuB，一个面向自我中心视觉观测下多类人机器人协作的仿真基准，包含10个任务、同步演示数据，并揭示现有多类人机器人协作策略的显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类环境中的许多物理任务需要协作，而现有类人机器人基准多聚焦单机器人技能，缺乏在自我中心视觉观测下对多类人机器人协作的评估。

Method: 构建CoHuB仿真基准：提供10个任务（8个双机器人、2个三机器人），覆盖多种协作模式；并通过多操作员VR遥操作管线采集同步演示数据，每个操作员从各自机器人的自我中心视角控制一个机器人。

Result: 使用代表性视觉运动策略进行实验，结果显示在不同形式的协同感知与控制上仍存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: CoHuB为开发与评估多类人机器人协作策略提供了基础平台。

Abstract: Many physical tasks in human environments require collaboration, from assisting a partner to jointly manipulating an object. Yet, existing humanoid benchmarks largely focus on single-humanoid skills and lack evaluation of multi-humanoid collaboration under egocentric visual observations. We introduce CoHuB (Collaborative Multi-Humanoid Benchmark), a simulation benchmark for multi-humanoid collaboration under egocentric visual observations. CoHuB provides 10 tasks, eight with two humanoids and two with three humanoids, spanning diverse collaboration patterns. We also provide synchronized demonstrations collected through a multi-operator VR teleoperation pipeline, in which each operator controls one humanoid from its egocentric view. Experiments with representative visuomotor policies reveal substantial challenges across different forms of coordinated perception and control. CoHuB provides a foundation for developing and evaluating multi-humanoid collaboration policies.

</details>


### [155] [AGRO-SUVIDE: Agentic Robotics for Surgical Viscoelastic Debridement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34823)
*Shutong Jin,Ziyang Chen,Preethi Satish,Medow Shen,Gary Guthart,Florian T. Pokorny,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向手术黏弹性组织清创的智能体机器人框架AGRO-SUVIDE，实现小碎片反复清除任务，单碎片清除成功率85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术中重复性操作易导致外科医生疲劳，增强灵巧性有潜力减轻疲劳。而黏弹性基底上附着的小碎片反复清除（SUVIDE）是一项典型的高重复性手术任务，亟需自动化方案。

Method: 采用模块化智能体框架，利用智能体的自我改进与编程能力：演示分析模块从单次专家演示中依据视觉与运动学信息自动识别可复用技能；构建模块将每项技能构建为基于模型的程序化技能（智能体针对脚手架库编写代码）或无模型的策略型技能；运行时监控模块按观测到的碎片数量将技能组合成循环式图，并校验各技能前后置条件以决定推进或重试。

Result: 在da Vinci研究套件（dVRK）上完成340次物理实验。单碎片清除平均成功率达85%，连续三碎片清除成功率为60%，加入一次人工干预后提升至95%；并可泛化至未见过的五碎片场景，单碎片清除平均成功率80%。

Conclusion: AGRO-SUVIDE验证了智能体机器人框架在手术黏弹性清创任务中的可行性与泛化能力，为提升手术自动化与减轻医生疲劳提供了新思路。

Abstract: Augmented dexterity has the potential to reduce the fatigue experienced by surgeons during repetitive surgical tasks. In this paper, we propose the first AGentic RObotics framework for SUrgical VIscoelastic DEbridement (AGRO-SUVIDE), the repeated removal of small fragments attached to a viscoelastic substrate. Leveraging the self-improving and coding capability of agents, AGRO-SUVIDE adopts a modular framework. Specifically, the demonstration analysis module automatically identifies recurring skills from a single expert demonstration, using both visual and kinematic information. The construction module then builds each skill, either as a procedural model-based skill the agent codes against a scaffolded library or as a model-free policy-based skill. At runtime, the monitoring module composes the skills into a loop-style graph sized to the number of fragments it observes, then verifies pre- and post-conditions of each skill to decide whether to advance or retry. We evaluate AGRO-SUVIDE through 340 physical trials on the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK). AGRO-SUVIDE achieves an average single-fragment removal success rate of 85%, completing consecutive three-fragment removal at 60% and at 95% with one human intervention. It further generalizes to unseen five-fragment scenarios with an average success rate of 80% for single-fragment removal. Project page: https://surgical-robotics.github.io/AGRO-SUVIDE/

</details>


### [156] [Don't Throw Away the Tail: Action Upcycling for Policy Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34911)
*Taesung Kwon,Jangho Park,Sunwoo Park,Youngmin Kim,Seonghyun Jin,Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的Action Upcycling算法，通过复用原本会被丢弃的动作来延长执行视界，在保持成功率的同时减少1.2-1.7倍的策略调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人策略从单次观测预测未来动作块，只执行前缀后丢弃剩余动作再重新规划。执行视界的选择存在反应性与效率的权衡：短视界保持反应性但需频繁调用策略；现有测试时自适应方法要么读取模型内部信号（需针对每种架构选择），要么额外采样增加成本。

Method: Action Upcycling是一种免训练算法，无需访问模型内部或额外采样即可复用原本被丢弃的动作。核心发现是：只要动作速度保持平滑，被丢弃的动作就与重新规划的版本相近。因此算法将执行视界延长至速度开始波动的那一点。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界操作任务上的大量实验表明，跨多种Vision-Language-Action模型（VLA）甚至World Action Model（WAM），Action Upcycling在无成功率损失的情况下将策略调用减少1.2-1.7倍。

Conclusion: 该方法可应用于任何分块策略，成本可忽略，且与少步采样、流式动作解码等其他策略加速方法正交，为策略加速开辟了新维度。

Abstract: Modern robot policies predict a chunk of future actions from a single observation, execute only a prefix, and discard the rest before replanning. Choosing the length of this prefix, the execution horizon, poses a trade-off between reactivity and efficiency. A short horizon keeps the policy reactive to the environment, but requires frequent policy calls. Recent test-time methods adaptively select the horizon for each chunk, but they either read model internals, where the signal must be chosen for each architecture, or draw extra samples, which adds cost. We propose *Action Upcycling*, a training-free algorithm that reuses actions the policy would otherwise discard, without accessing model internals or drawing extra samples. We find that discarded actions stay close to their replanned versions as long as the action velocity remains smooth. Action Upcycling therefore extends the execution horizon up to the point where the velocity begins to fluctuate. Extensive experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation tasks show that Action Upcycling reduces policy calls by 1.2--1.7$\times$ with no loss in success rate, across multiple Vision-Language-Action Models (VLAs) and even a World Action Model (WAM). It applies to any chunked policy at negligible cost and is orthogonal to other policy acceleration methods such as few-step sampling and streaming action decoding, opening a new axis for policy acceleration.

</details>


### [157] [RoboFL: Federated Expert Assembly for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34968)
*Rongyu Zhang,Ruizhi Fan,Yunfan Lou,Hengyu Fang,Shenli Zheng,Chenrui Wu,Yili Jin,Li Du,Dan Wang,Yuan Du,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboFL 提出 MoSAIC 框架，将本地训练的 LoRA 适配器作为服务器端 MoE 的专家分支，实现联邦世界-动作模型学习，在多个基准和真实机器人上取得领先性能并大幅降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）和世界-动作模型受限于物理交互数据稀缺、机构间数据孤岛以及任务异构等问题。联邦学习是自然的解决思路，通过参数高效微调（PEFT）让各客户端适配共享基础模型，避免交换全模型更新。但联邦化这些适配器并不简单：朴素聚合会混淆不兼容的更新，而将 MoE 式路由引入联邦聚合可能稀释专业化并破坏专家选择的稳定性。

Method: 提出 RoboFL，实例化 MoSAIC（Mixture of Slotted Adapters）用于联邦世界-动作学习。MoSAIC 直接将本地训练的 LoRA 适配器安装为服务器 MoE 的专家分支，服务器端路由器在这些先验信息指导下学习 token 分配，并联合优化路由与专家参数。FARD（Foresight-to-Action Routing Distillation）对模型三条路径的路由进行对齐；PCEA（Path-Consensus Expert Aggregation）将完整的专家更新转换为紧凑的全局适配器，用于个性化再分发。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0、RLBench 以及真实 Franka 机械臂上的实验表明，RoboFL 通过结构化专家组装具有优越性：在 Franka 机械臂上比集中式 PEFT InternVLA-A1 高出 12.23%，同时相比基于 MoE 的联邦 VLA 基线，每轮客户端通信量最多减少 86.81%。

Conclusion: RoboFL 通过 MoSAIC 与配套的 FARD、PCEA 机制，有效解决了联邦 VLA/世界-动作模型中的适配器聚合难题，在提升性能的同时显著降低通信成本，验证了结构化专家组装在联邦世界-动作学习中的价值。

Abstract: Vision-language-action and world-action models are increasingly popular, yet remain bottlenecked by physical interaction data that is scarce, institutionally siloed, and task-heterogeneous. A natural federated solution is to let each client adapt a shared foundation model through parameter-efficient fine-tuning, avoiding the exchange of full-model updates. However, federating these adapters is nontrivial, as naive aggregation can entangle incompatible updates, while incorporating MoE-style routing into federated aggregation may dilute specialization and destabilize expert selection. We present RoboFL, which instantiates MoSAIC (Mixture of Slotted Adapters) for federated world-action learning. MoSAIC directly installs locally trained LoRA adapters as the expert branches of a server MoE. Server-side routers learn token assignments over these prior-informed branches while jointly refining routing and expert parameters. Foresight-to-Action Routing Distillation (FARD) aligns routing across the model's three paths, while Path-Consensus Expert Aggregation (PCEA) converts complete expert updates into a compact global adapter for personalized redistribution. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0, RLBench, and a real-world Franka robot arm show the superiority of RoboFL with structured expert assembly, as it outperforms centralized PEFT InternVLA-A1 by 12.23% on the Franka arm, while reducing per-round client communication by up to 86.81% relative to MoE-based federated VLA baselines.

</details>


### [158] [NavJev: Efficient Vision-Language Navigation via Action-Centric Visual Compression and Discriminative Action-Semantic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34969)
*Kai Sheng,Liuyi Wang,Jinlong Li,Haojie Dai,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavJev 是一个高效的零样本视觉语言导航框架，将在线导航从重复的多模态生成重构为紧凑视觉压缩加轻量类型化动作选择，在 R2R-CE 上以 0.65 秒/步达到 27.0% SR 和 22.4% SPL，显著降低推理延迟与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本视觉语言导航方法依赖多模态大语言模型对视觉观测、导航指令和候选动作进行推理，虽有效但在每一步都调用自回归多模态推理，导致推理延迟高，限制了具身智能体的响应能力。

Method: 提出 NavJev 框架，包含两部分：动作中心视觉压缩（ACVC）将路点几何、BLIP 字幕和 RAM 语义标签集成到候选动作的紧凑表示中；判别式动作语义记忆（DASM）过滤共享语义并跨导航步骤保留有判别性的动作特有证据。基于这些表示，Jev 直接对可用动作集进行结构化概率决策。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 数据集上，NavJev 达到 27.0% 的成功率（SR）和 22.4% 的 SPL，每步导航仅需 0.65 秒，相比基于 MLLM 的 VLN 方法大幅降低推理延迟与成本。

Conclusion: NavJev 通过将重复的多模态生成转化为紧凑视觉压缩与轻量类型化动作选择，在保持有效导航性能的同时显著提升推理效率，为具身智能体的实时响应提供了可行方案。项目页面见 https://kai-sheng-caesar.github.io/NavJev/。

Abstract: Recent zero-shot Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) methods increasingly rely on multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to reason over visual observations, navigation instructions, and candidate actions. Although effective, repeatedly invoking autoregressive multimodal reasoning at every navigation step introduces substantial inference latency, limiting the responsiveness of embodied agents. We propose NavJev, an efficient VLN framework that reformulates online navigation from repeated multimodal generation into compact visual compression followed by lightweight typed action selection. Specifically, Action-Centric Visual Compression (ACVC) integrates waypoint geometry, BLIP captions, and RAM semantic tags into compact representations of candidate actions, while Discriminative Action-Semantic Memory (DASM) filters shared semantics and maintains discriminative action-specific evidence across navigation steps. Based on these representations, Jev directly performs structured probabilistic decisions over the available action set. Experiments on R2R-CE show that NavJev achieves 27.0% SR and 22.4% SPL with only 0.65 s per navigation step, while substantially reducing inference latency and cost compared with MLLM-based VLN methods. The project page is available at https://kai-sheng-caesar.github.io/NavJev/.

</details>


### [159] [Graph-Based Simultaneous Path and Foothold Planning for Multi-Limbed Intra-Vehicular Robots in Space Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35000)
*Masazumi Imai,Kentaro Uno,Toshinori Kuwahara,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图论的多肢空间站内机器人同步路径与落脚点规划框架，在三维国际空间站舱室模型中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间站内机器人作业对减轻宇航员负担、提升在轨活动效率至关重要。配备抓取末端执行器的多肢舱内机器人（MLIVR）可抓握扶手、座椅导轨等既有接口，实现微重力下稳定移动与有力操作。但这些接口上可抓握位置在空间上有限且离散分布，因此MLIVR的运动规划必须与落脚点规划联合进行。作者旨在解决这一路径与落脚点耦合规划问题。

Method: 提出一种基于图论的同步路径与落脚点规划框架。该方法在满足可操作度约束的前提下，高效搜索多肢机器人可行的支撑序列（stance sequences）。

Result: 在国际空间站（ISS）舱室三维模型的仿真中验证了框架的有效性，能够生成可行且高效的舱内移动规划方案。

Conclusion: 该框架可在真实舱内环境中为多肢机器人生成可行且高效的移动规划，证明了同步路径与落脚点规划方法的实用性。

Abstract: Robot-aided operations in space stations are essential for reducing the workload of astronauts and improving the efficiency of on-orbit activities. Multi-limbed intra-vehicular robots (MLIVRs) equipped with grappling end-effectors have emerged as a promising solution, as they can securely grasp pre-existing interfaces, such as handrails and seat tracks, thereby enabling stable locomotion and forceful manipulation in microgravity environments. Since graspable locations on these interfaces are spatially limited and discretely distributed, motion planning for MLIVRs must be addressed jointly with foothold planning. This paper presents a simultaneous path and foothold planning framework based on graph theory for MLIVRs. The proposed method efficiently searches for feasible stance sequences for a multi-limbed robot while satisfying manipulability constraints. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated through simulations in a 3D model of the International Space Station (ISS) cabin, demonstrating its capability to generate feasible and efficient locomotion plans in realistic intra-vehicular environments.

</details>


### [160] [Learning to Act under Visual Interruptions with Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35003)
*Mingle Jiang,Rui Xu,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型在相机中断情况下的闭环操作能力,提出MAIL-Bench基准和MINT方法,显著提升相机丢失下的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常在任务执行全程拥有所有相机流的条件下开发与评估,当某相机在执行中停止提供帧时,策略必须继续行动。然而这种中断如何影响闭环操作尚缺乏充分理解,具有重要实际意义。

Method: MAIL-Bench通过在每条成功参考轨迹的多个阶段中断不同相机,评估策略在视觉输入不可用时的能力保持。MINT先训练VLA策略在缺失视觉输入下保持功能;推理时选择性使用光流外推或动作条件世界模型补充缺失观测,并在预测视图不可靠时撤除。

Result: 在π₀.₅和GR00T N1.5上的实验表明,MINT在相机丢失下显著提升任务成功率。在AgiBot G2上的实验进一步验证了真实机器人部署。

Conclusion: MINT能有效提升VLA模型在相机中断情况下的鲁棒性与任务成功率,并具备真实机器人部署能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, but they are typically developed and evaluated with all camera streams available throughout task execution. When a camera stops delivering frames during task execution, the policy must continue acting without access to subsequent observations from the missing view. Despite its practical importance, how such interruptions affect closed-loop manipulation remains insufficiently understood. To investigate this problem, we introduce MAIL-Bench, a benchmark that evaluates visual interruptions with VLA models. By interrupting different cameras at multiple stages of each policy's successful reference trajectory, MAIL-Bench measures how well policies retain their capabilities when visual inputs become unavailable. Building on this benchmark, we propose MINT, which first trains VLA policies to remain functional under missing visual inputs. At inference time, MINT selectively supplements missing observations using optical-flow extrapolation or an action-conditioned world model, and withdraws predicted views when they become unreliable. Experiments on $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.5 show that MINT significantly improves task success under camera loss over the original models. Experiments on AgiBot G2 further demonstrate the real-robot deployment under camera loss. The benchmark is available at https://minglejiang.github.io/Mail-Bench/

</details>


### [161] [Do Not Cut When Uncertain: Rejectable and Calibrated Decision Heads for VLA Policies in Robotic Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35039)
*Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RCDH可拒绝校准决策头，替换VLA输出接口，在遮挡场景下实现风险感知弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略经行为克隆或流匹配训练后必须输出动作轨迹，无法表达"我不知道"或"不应行动"。在机器人采摘中遮挡导致单帧决策本质歧义：相同像素可能对应可切割茎或无茎，现有VLA被迫做出承诺，产生高置信度且后果不可逆的错误。作者主张VLA失败模式由输出接口而非主干规模决定。

Method: 提出Rejectable and Calibrated Decision Heads（RCDH），一种类型化、可拒绝、校准的输出接口，可附加于冻结的VLA主干而无需重训练或新特征。包含两部分：(i) 带显式拒绝与有序条件分解的决策模式；(ii) 面向风险感知弃权的校准流程。在可控叶片遮挡的机器人采摘平台上评估，比较生成式、枚举式、校准式与可拒绝式接口。

Result: 仅替换输出头即可在遮挡下恢复分布外可用性，同时保持分布内性能。进一步检验拒绝空间排序是否关键。

Conclusion: 拒绝的权利，而非更大的模型，是不确定性下可靠操作所缺失的接口。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies trained with behavior cloning or flow matching are optimized to output an action trajectory, but they cannot express "I don't know" or "I should not act." In robotic harvesting, occlusion makes single-frame decisions fundamentally ambiguous: identical pixels can correspond either to a cuttable stem or to no stem at all. Existing VLAs are forced to commit, leading to high-confidence errors with irreversible consequences. We argue that the failure mode of a VLA is determined not by backbone scale but by its output interface. We propose Rejectable and Calibrated Decision Heads (RCDH), a typed, rejectable, and calibrated output interface that can be attached to a frozen VLA backbone without retraining or new features. RCDH introduces (i) a decision schema with explicit rejection and ordered, conditional decomposition, and (ii) a calibration procedure for risk-aware abstention. We evaluate RCDH on a robotic harvesting platform with controllable leaf occlusion, comparing generative, enumerated, calibrated, and rejectable interfaces. We show that replacing only the output head restores out-of-distribution usability under occlusion while preserving in-distribution performance. We further test whether the ordering of the rejection space is critical. Our results suggest that the right to refuse, rather than a larger model, is the missing interface for reliable manipulation under uncertainty.

</details>


### [162] [EMPIRIC: Experiment-Driven Learning of Residual World Models for Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35047)
*Yichao Liang,Amber Li,Dat Nguyen,Emily Bunnapradist,Michelangelo Naim,Sreela Kodali,Matteo Merler,Bowen Li,Kiran Gopinathan,Yiyun Liu,Nikhil Pimpalkhare,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Adrian Weller,Zenna Tavares,Tom Silver,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMPIRIC让机器人通过实验学习"残差世界模型"：在物理引擎基础上用程序补全缺失机制（如胶水固化、水加热、风），并用贝叶斯推断从噪声观测中估计参数与隐藏状态，从而实现预测、主动实验与假设修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理引擎虽提供运动与接触知识，但可能整体遗漏某些机制（胶水固化、水加热、风），导致机器人无法准确预测与规划；而完全从零学习又过于低效。因此需要一种能复用引擎已有知识、仅补充缺失部分的方法。

Method: 提出EMPIRIC智能体，学习"残差世界模型"：以物理引擎为基座，用可学习的程序代码表示缺失机制，程序可引入新的力、约束与隐藏状态；再通过贝叶斯推断从含噪观测中估计这些参数与隐藏状态；模型支持预测动作结果、选择高信息量实验，并在预测失败时修正假设。

Result: 在五个仿真域中，EMPIRIC学到可解释、可复用的模型，并以更少的环境交互解决更多任务，优于全部三个基线；在真实机器人上，它学会风力与多米诺质量以完成操作任务。

Conclusion: 将物理引擎与可学习程序化残差机制及贝叶斯推断结合，能高效、可解释地让机器人获得新机制知识并用于规划，且可迁移到真实机器人。

Abstract: A robot should be able to learn through experiments how unfamiliar objects behave and interact, then plan with that knowledge. It need not start from scratch: physics engines supply knowledge of motion and contact, but can omit entire mechanisms, such as glue curing, water heating, or wind. We present EMPIRIC, an agent that learns a residual world model: a physics engine extended with code for the missing mechanisms. The learned programs can introduce new forces, constraints, and hidden state, and Bayesian inference estimates their parameters and states from noisy observations. The resulting model lets the agent predict the outcomes of actions, choose informative experiments, and revise its hypotheses when predictions fail. Across five simulated domains, EMPIRIC learns interpretable, reusable models, and solves more tasks with fewer environment interactions than all three baselines. On a physical robot, it learns wind forces and domino masses to solve a manipulation task. Website and code: https://yichao-liang.github.io/empiric

</details>


### [163] [QuadHand: A Compact Quadrotor Aerial Manipulator with MRC-SDF-Based Whole-Body Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35094)
*Rui Jin,Ruiyang Liu,Xinhang Xu,Haotian Jin,Yi Wang,Yizhuo Yang,Lihua Xi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QuadHand紧凑型四旋翼空中机械臂系统，配备3自由度机械臂、夹爪及电池辅助被动重心补偿模块，并设计MRC-SDF多关节机器人中心符号距离场与时空全身轨迹优化框架，实现复杂环境中安全可执行的空中操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机机械臂（UAM）将机械臂与空中平台结合以实现三维物理交互，但增大工作空间会加剧机械臂引起的扰动；同时现有几何表示在近距离交互中难以兼顾几何保真度与计算效率。

Method: 提出QuadHand系统（紧凑四旋翼+3自由度机械臂+夹爪+电池辅助被动重心补偿模块）；提出MRC-SDF多关节机器人中心符号距离场，兼顾细节与可计算性；构建时空全身轨迹优化框架，联合优化四旋翼与机械臂以生成安全可执行轨迹。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，该系统能在复杂环境中实现安全且可执行的空中操作。

Conclusion: 通过被动重心补偿与全身轨迹优化，QuadHand有效缓解机械臂引起的扰动，MRC-SDF在保真度与效率间取得平衡，验证了复杂环境下空中操作的安全性与可执行性。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial manipulators (UAMs) integrate robotic arms with aerial platforms for three-dimensional physical interaction. However, enlarging the workspace increases arm-induced disturbances, while existing geometric representations face a trade-off between geometric fidelity and computational efficiency in close-proximity interaction. This paper presents QuadHand, a compact quadrotor aerial manipulator with a 3-DoF arm, gripper, and battery-assisted passive CoG compensation module to reduce dominant arm-induced disturbances. We further propose MRC-SDF, a Multi-articulated Robot-Centric Signed Distance Field that preserves fine geometric detail with tractable computation, and a spatiotemporal whole-body trajectory optimization framework that jointly optimizes the quadrotor and manipulator for safe and executable trajectory generation. Simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate safe and executable aerial manipulation in complex environments.

</details>


### [164] [ReCAT: Remember, Count, and Time: Structured Recurrent Memory for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35200)
*Pankhuri Vanjani,Mostafa Hatab,Can Mizrakli,Vaisakh Shaj,Zhuoyue Li,Moritz Reuss,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReCAT：一种带结构化循环记忆的语言条件机器人策略，用Mamba-2循环记忆加因果注意力编码历史观测，配合flow-matching Transformer解码器逐块跨注意力读取历史表征，在LIBERO、RMBench与三类真实机器人记忆任务上显著优于短历史基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆依赖型操作要求机器人依据当前传感器已无法获取的信息做决策，如回忆早期视觉线索、跟踪任务进度、计数重复事件或估计已流逝时间。现有策略多依赖短历史或缺乏结构化记忆，难以胜任这类任务，因此需要一个具备结构化循环记忆的语言条件策略。

Method: ReCAT由三部分组成：指令条件编码器从当前观测中提取特征；循环记忆模块通过Mamba-2层与一层因果注意力整合观测流；flow-matching Transformer解码器在每个块中分别用交叉注意力读取当前表征与历史表征。作者还对比了不同记忆更新规则（加法更新、delta-rule更新）作为机器人记忆的效果。

Result: 在LIBERO上平均成功率95.3%，在RMBench上62.4%，九个任务中有六个取得最佳或并列最佳。在探测空间回忆、事件计数和时间间隔的三项真实机器人任务上，最佳ReCAT变体平均成功率66.7%，而最强短历史基线仅8.3%。消融显示观测编码器与逐块记忆条件化均为性能所必需；加法更新在计数与计时上成功率最高，delta-rule更新在空间回忆上更好。

Conclusion: 结构化循环记忆与逐块历史条件化是记忆依赖型机器人操作的关键，且针对高效序列建模设计的更新规则在作为机器人记忆时表现各异，需按任务类型选择。

Abstract: Memory-dependent manipulation requires robots to make decisions using information that is no longer available to their current sensors, such as recalling an earlier visual cue, tracking task progress, counting repeated events, or estimating elapsed time. We present ReCAT, a language-conditioned policy with structured recurrent memory. An instruction-conditioned encoder forms features from the current observation. A recurrent memory integrates the observation stream through Mamba-2 layers and one causal attention layer. A flow-matching Transformer decoder reads the current and the historical representation through separate cross-attention in every block. ReCAT reaches 95.3\% average success on LIBERO and 62.4\% on RMBench, with the best or tied-best result on six of nine tasks. On three real-robot tasks probing spatial recall, event counting, and interval timing, the best ReCAT variant reaches 66.7\% average success, against 8.3\% for the strongest short-history baseline. Controlled comparisons within ReCAT show that the observation encoder and every-block memory conditioning are needed for this performance. They also show that update rules developed for efficient sequence modeling behave differently as robot memory: additive updates have the highest observed success on counting and timing, and delta-rule updates on spatial recall. Project website is at https://intuitive-robots.github.io/ReCAT

</details>


### [165] [Zero-Shot Reactive Obstacle Avoidance for Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35231)
*Weihang Guo,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的实时避障方法 NUDGE，通过可微分几何将符号距离场梯度注入扩散/流匹配策略的推理过程，支持多种动作参数化并保持原策略任务分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略与视觉-语言-动作模型等在机器人任务中表现优异，但缺乏可靠的避障能力；作者希望在不重新训练的前提下，为任意此类策略赋予实时、反应式的避障功能，且不破坏原有任务分布。

Method: NUDGE（Nudge Update via Differentiable GEometry）是一种免训练的避障流程，可嵌入任意基于扩散或流匹配的机器人策略。核心做法是在推理时把符号距离场（返回每点到最近障碍距离的函数）的梯度注入策略采样过程，从而将生成的动作轨迹推离障碍。为兼容多种动作参数化（绝对/相对关节位姿、末端执行器位姿等），引入可微分关节轨迹解码器进行统一映射。

Result: 实验表明 NUDGE 能在保持策略原有任务分布的同时实现反应式实时运行。

Conclusion: NUDGE 为扩散/流匹配类机器人策略提供了一种即插即用、免训练、实时的避障方案，兼容多种动作表示，具备较强通用性与实用价值。

Abstract: We propose NUDGE (Nudge Update via Differentiable GEometry), a training-free obstacle-avoidance procedure that can be incorporated in any robot policy based on diffusion or flow matching, including diffusion policies and vision-language-action models. Our work injects gradients from a signed distance field, a function returning each point's distance to the nearest obstacle, into the policy at inference time to steer it away from obstacles. It supports any common action parameterization, from absolute or relative joint poses to end-effector poses, through a differentiable joint-trajectory decoder. Experiments show that NUDGE preserves the policy's task distribution and runs reactively in real time.

</details>


### [166] [Spatial Grafting: Grounding 3D Features for Flow-Matching Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35249)
*Dingsheng Liu,Yangzheng Wu,Mahboubeh Asadi,Zhiyuan Li,Jinbang Huang,Yixin Xiao,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Spatial Grafting，一种轻量空间模块，将冻结的重建特征绑定到机器人相对度量几何，并以即插即用方式增强预训练操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人操作策略（VLA、WAM）未显式建模交互相关的度量几何；重建特征只描述局部形状，缺乏机器人相对位置信息，且如何有效注入预训练策略尚不明确。

Method: Spatial Grafting 构建度量接地的空间 token，通过交叉注意力注入 flow-matching 动作专家，不修改宿主感知通路，保留预训练优势；单一架构无需按宿主重新设计。

Result: 在 2 个 VLA 与 2 个 WAM、四个仿真基准及三个真实平台上验证。RoboTwin 2.0 上 Grafted π0.5 达 94.0%/92.4%，优于 WAM4D；BEHAVIOR-1K 六任务中五项超过 2025 挑战赛冠军，最高提升 0.47 Q-score。

Conclusion: Spatial Grafting 可作为通用、轻量的空间增强模块，持续提升多种预训练策略在短时、杂乱、长时程及真实机器人任务中的表现。

Abstract: Pretrained robot manipulation policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) or world-action models (WAMs) leave interaction-relevant metric geometry implicit. Recent breakthroughs in spatial reconstruction can supply the necessary geometry reliably, but their features describe local shape without stating where it lies with respect to the robot. How best to deliver these features to a pretrained policy remains unresolved. We propose Spatial Grafting, a versatile, lightweight spatial module that binds frozen reconstruction features to metric, robot-relative geometry. Spatial Grafting constructs metric-grounded spatial tokens and injects them into the flow-matching action expert through cross-attention, without modifying the host's perceptual pathway, so the host retains the full benefit of its pretraining. We evaluate it more broadly than any geometry-aware policy we compare against: one graft architecture, with no per-host redesign, on two VLAs and two WAMs, across four simulation benchmarks that span short-horizon manipulation, visual robustness, clutter and long-horizon mobile manipulation, and on three real-robot platforms with single- and dual-arm configurations. On RoboTwin 2.0, a dual-arm manipulation benchmark, the graft improves every host across VLAs and WAMs. Grafted $π_{0.5}$ gains 11.3% and 15.6% on clean and randomized scenes, reaching 94.0% and 92.4%, above the strongest published 3D-conditioned policy, WAM4D (93.8% and 89.9%). The margin widens as the horizon lengthens: on tasks from BEHAVIOR-1K, a dual-arm mobile manipulation challenge scored by average task progress, it surpasses the 2025 challenge winner on five of six tasks,by up to 0.47 Q-score, and exceeds a map-conditioned spatial policy on average across the three tasks both report.

</details>


### [167] [Repair Before You Fuse: Frozen-Host Adaptation for Corrupted-but-Present Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35264)
*Gia-Huy Thai,Quang-Thinh Ly,Anh-Minh Phan,Tuan Dang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出边界特征修复（BFR）框架，在冻结的主检测器上学习模态接口处的任务监督残差修正，无需干净参考、损坏元数据或在线更新即可提升相机-LiDAR检测器在损坏数据下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机-LiDAR检测器即使两个传感器都存在、同步且已标定，仍会继续使用不可靠的特征。论文旨在解决"存在但被损坏"的传感场景下的鲁棒性问题，而不是传感器缺失问题。

Method: BFR是一种冻结主机的适配框架，在检测器已消费的模态接口处学习任务监督的残差修正。BFR-C修复融合读取的每个相机特征层级；BFR-L将主机条件化的LiDAR候选对齐到选定边界，并相对冻结锚点路由逐站点创新；两者联合训练得到BFR-CL。零初始化的逐通道缩放使各变体在优化前精确为检测器级恒等映射，仅修复模块训练，编码器、融合消费者、路由器、检测头和主机归一化统计保持冻结。推理时无需干净参考、损坏元数据、时序历史或在线更新。

Result: 在完整的20种损坏、5个严重度的KITTI-C网格上，相对复现的冻结基线，BFR-C将MVX-Net的RCE从14.07降至11.92，将Focals Conv-F的RCE从14.29降至11.00。在后者主机上，BFR-L将AP_cor从73.65提升至74.48，BFR-CL达到77.01 AP_cor、10.46 RCE和86.02干净AP。在nuScenes-R上，BFR-CL将复现的MoME基线mAP鲁棒性比从80.1提升至81.4。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了边界修复作为一种针对性改造方法，可在不重新训练已部署检测器的情况下，改善"存在但被损坏"的传感鲁棒性。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR detectors can continue to consume unreliable features even when both sensors remain present, synchronized, and calibrated. We introduce \emph{Boundary Feature Repair} (BFR), a frozen-host adaptation framework that learns task-supervised residual corrections at modality interfaces the detector already consumes. BFR-C repairs each camera feature level read by fusion, whereas BFR-L aligns host-conditioned LiDAR candidates to a selected boundary and routes site-wise innovations relative to the frozen anchor. Their jointly trained composition is BFR-CL. Zero-initialized per-channel scales make every variant an exact detector-level identity before optimization; only the repair modules train, while the encoders, fusion consumer, router, detection head, and host normalization statistics remain fixed. At inference, BFR requires neither clean references, corruption metadata, temporal history, nor online updates. Across the complete 20-corruption, five-severity KITTI-C grid, BFR-C reduces RCE from $14.07$ to $11.92$ on MVX-Net and from $14.29$ to $11.00$ on Focals Conv-F relative to their reproduced frozen baselines. On the latter host, BFR-L raises AP$_{\mathrm{cor}}$ from $73.65$ to $74.48$, while BFR-CL reaches $77.01$ AP$_{\mathrm{cor}}$ and $10.46$ RCE with $86.02$ clean AP. On nuScenes-R, BFR-CL raises the reproduced MoME baseline's mAP robustness ratio from $80.1$ to $81.4$. These results establish boundary repair as a targeted retrofit for corrupted-but-present sensing without retraining the deployed detector.

</details>


### [168] [GuardPIBT: Counterfactually Gated Neural Guidance for Ultra-Large-Scale 3D Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35267)
*Yuan Zhou,Zhenyu Hou,Guangtong Xu,Xiaoqiang Ji,Yuqing Tang,Jialiang Hou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 GuardPIBT，一种在 PIBT 执行器框架上叠加神经预测的混合方法，用于大规模密集交通下的多智能体路径规划（MAPF）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 3D 多智能体路径规划在密集交通下难度显著上升；PIBT 虽可扩展性好，但其单步目标导向排序在密集交互和大规模拥堵下不够充分。

Method: 采用增强而非替代 PIBT 的思路：神经预测仅对 PIBT 原生候选解提出残差重排序，最终动作仍由 PIBT 决定。具体包括：(1) 局部图注意力建模近邻交互，全局源—目标运输特征提供群体级协调上下文；(2) 反事实群组门控过滤可能降低协调性的重排序；(3) 面向超大规模群体引入种群自适应分组、异步缓存推理和选择性修复。PIBT 始终保留有效性检查、优先继承与回溯。

Result: 在多达 10 万智能体的实验中，2D 与 3D 环境均能可靠完成规划，包括全部三次 10 万智能体仓库运行且经审计的图违规为零。

Conclusion: GuardPIBT 能在保持 PIBT 正确性保证的同时，借助神经重排序与规模化机制，实现超大规模密集交通场景下的可靠多智能体路径规划。

Abstract: Large-scale 3D multi-agent path finding becomes increasingly difficult under dense traffic. Priority Inheritance with Backtracking (PIBT) scales well, but its one-step goal-directed ordering may become insufficient under dense interactions and large-scale congestion. We present GuardPIBT, which augments rather than replaces the PIBT executor: neural predictions only propose residual reorderings of PIBT's native candidates, while final actions remain determined by PIBT. First, local graph attention models nearby interactions, while global source--goal transport features provide population-level coordination context for candidate reordering. Second, a counterfactual group gate filters reorderings whose closed-loop effects may degrade coordination. Third, for ultra-large populations, population-adaptive grouping preserves decision granularity, asynchronous cached inference amortizes neural computation, and selective repair resolves long-tail agents. PIBT retains validity checking, priority inheritance, and backtracking throughout. Experiments with up to 100,000 agents demonstrate reliable completion across 2D and 3D environments, including all three 100,000-agent warehouse runs with zero audited graph violations. The project website is available at {\color{magenta}\texttt{https://guardpibt.github.io/GuardPIBT/}}.

</details>


### [169] [DexAgent: An Agentic Human2Sim2Robot Framework for Dexterous Manipulation with Self-Evolving Tool Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35318)
*Youhui Wang,Yunzhu Li,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Huang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexAgent是一个智能体式的Human2Sim2Robot框架，将单段第一人称人类视频和任务提示转化为物理可行的机器人轨迹，用于训练灵巧操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Human2Sim2Robot流程依赖预定义程序，难以适应多样的物体属性与交互，尤其是涉及铰接和可变形物体的情况。

Method: 框架分四个阶段：人类视频语义理解、基于属性的仿真重建、机器人轨迹优化、机器人数据生成。每个阶段通过工具库选择或开发合适技能，并用属性专属验证器评估结果的物理有效性与任务要求、反馈精修以阻止误差传播。最终阶段在仿真中变化物体和机器人状态生成多样轨迹，并对渲染观测重纹理以促进sim-to-real迁移，新技能和验证器保留于工具库实现自演化。

Result: 在十一个真实世界任务中，使用DexAgent生成数据训练的策略成功率比竞争基线高3.5倍。

Conclusion: 自适应、验证引导的流程使DexAgent能处理多样物体和长时程任务，且随处理视频增多而不断积累可复用能力、缩短处理时间。

Abstract: Human videos offer a scalable source of demonstrations for dexterous robot manipulation. However, existing human-to-simulation-to-robot (Human2Sim2Robot) pipelines rely on predefined procedures that struggle to accommodate diverse object properties and interactions, particularly those involving articulated and deformable objects. We introduce DexAgent, an agentic Human2Sim2Robot framework that converts a single egocentric human video and a task prompt into physically grounded robot trajectories for policy training. It operates through four stages: semantic understanding of human videos, property-based simulation reconstruction, robot trajectory optimization, and robot data generation. At each stage, DexAgent adapts its approach to the task and object properties by selecting suitable skills from its tool library or developing new ones when needed. Property-specific verifiers assess stage outcomes for physical validity and task-specific requirements and provide feedback for refinement, preventing error propagation through the workflow. This adaptive, verification-guided process allows DexAgent to process diverse objects and long-horizon tasks. In the final stage, DexAgent varies object and robot states in simulation to generate diverse robot trajectories from a single human video, then retextures the rendered observations to facilitate sim-to-real transfer. Newly developed skills and verifiers are retained in its tool library, making it self-evolving to accumulate reusable capabilities. This reduces processing time as DexAgent encounters more human videos. Across eleven real-world tasks, policies trained with DexAgent-generated data achieve a 3.5x higher success rate than competing baselines. Project website: https://dexagent123.github.io/.

</details>


### [170] [From Pixel to Poses: Object-centric Tool Manipulation Learning from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35375)
*Bangjun Wang,Longyan Wu,Yukun Wei,Shenghe Shao,Chaoyi Huang,Wenze Cui,Zetong Xu,Hanlin Wu,Long Chen,Yi Ma,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: P2P-T：从像素到位姿的工具操控框架，无需人机对齐数据，仅用人类演示即可学习，任务性能较SOTA提升73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操控扩展受限于真实机器人数据稀缺。现有借助人类视频的方法计算开销大，且仍需配对的人机数据做域对齐；同时现有SOTA擅长长程任务，却难以胜任复杂工具操控所需的精细精准控制。

Method: 提出数据高效、以物体为中心的两阶段框架P2P-T。第一阶段预训练物体中心世界模型以提取稳定的位姿先验；第二阶段将先验整合进高效、位姿感知的底层策略。借助现代基础模型驱动的自动化数据处理管线，完全绕过对人机对齐数据的需求，显著降低训练开销。

Result: 在复杂真实工具操控任务上，仅需极少量的每任务微调，执行性能较此前SOTA提升73%，而这些任务对标准大规模预训练模型仍难以实现。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心的位姿先验学习可从人类演示直接迁移至机器人工具操控，避免人机对齐数据与高计算成本，为数据高效的精细操控提供可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling up robotic manipulation is primarily bottlenecked by the scarcity of real-world robot data. While recent approaches leverage human video demonstrations to mitigate this shortage, they remain computationally expensive and still rely on paired human-robot data for domain alignment. Although current state-of-the-arts excel at long-horizon tasks, they struggle with the delicate and precise control required for complex tool manipulation. To overcome these limitations, we introduce P2P-T, from Pixel to Poses for Tool Manipulation, a data-efficient, object-centric framework that learns tool use directly from human demonstrations. P2P-T bridges the cognitive and physical execution gap through a two-stage approach. First, pretraining an object-centric world model to extract stable pose priors; second, integrating these priors into an efficient, pose-aware low-level policy. By utilizing a robust automated data processing pipeline powered by modern foundation models, P2P-T completely bypasses the need for human-robot aligned data. This reduces overall training overhead drastically. With minimal per-task fine-tuning, our framework achieves a 73% improvement over the previous state of the art in execution performance on complex, real-world tool manipulation tasks that currently remain out of reach for standard large-scale pretrained models.

</details>


### [171] [Adaptive Safety Filtering for Frozen ACC Policies via Conformal Residual Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35415)
*Zhiruo Zhou,Rigaudiere Z. Li,Chen Xiwen,Yucheng Chen,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出残差感知保形动作过滤（RACF），通过在部署中校准固定标称预测器的残差并转化为操作裕度，实现无需重训练的自适应约束满足，在2400个控制器-试验单元中达到94.3%回合安全率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的ACC策略在部署动态与训练条件不一致时容易违反约束，需要一种无需重新训练即可在线适应并保证安全的方法。

Method: RACF校准固定标称预测器的残差，将其分位数转化为有限模型动作投影的操作裕度；已完成的状态转移可更新裕度与候选选择而无需重训练策略。通过受控实验隔离残差裕度注入的效果，并刻画一步残差覆盖与约束满足之间的关系。

Result: 在2400个控制器-试验单元的注册比较中，Adaptive RACF达到94.3%回合安全性，较标称CBF-QP基线提升19.9个百分点，投影频率从8.11%降至6.63%；受控研究隔离出残差裕度注入带来4.54点提升。在匹配硬件评估中，相比Robust CBF-QP平均摊销rollout时间减少21.2%，安全回合为161/180对170/180。

Conclusion: RACF能在不重训练的情况下自适应提升约束满足，并量化了安全性与计算开销之间的权衡，但其匹配硬件评估中安全回合数略低于Robust CBF-QP，显示存在安全-效率折衷。

Abstract: Frozen adaptive cruise control (ACC) policies can violate constraints when deployment dynamics differ from their training conditions. We propose residual-aware conformal action filtering (RACF), which calibrates residuals of a fixed nominal predictor and converts their quantile into an operating margin for finite-model action projection. Completed transitions update margins and candidate selection without retraining the policy. In a registered comparison over 2,400 controller-trial units, Adaptive RACF achieves 94.3% episode safety, improving by 19.9 percentage points over the evaluated nominal CBF-QP baseline while reducing projection frequency from 8.11% to 6.63%. A controlled study isolates a 4.54-point improvement from residual-margin injection. In a separate matched-hardware evaluation, Adaptive reduces mean amortized rollout time by 21.2% relative to Robust CBF-QP, with 161/180 versus 170/180 safe episodes. We characterize conditions linking one-step residual coverage to constraint satisfaction and quantify the observed safety-computation trade-offs.

</details>


### [172] [Memory in the Sky: Low-Altitude Question Answering with Multi-Agent Memory Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35431)
*Chengyang Li,Yujie Wan,Shuai Wang,Kejiang Ye,Weijie Yuan,Boyu Zhou,Yik-Chung Wu,Chengzhong Xu,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向低空问答（LAQA）任务的记忆中心框架MemCen，用生成对抗考试（GAE）量化记忆价值，并联合选择无人机与分配功率以最大化记忆质量，在仿真与真实实验中均取得较高问答精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统资源分配基于感知、通信、控制或计算指标，但LAQA任务需要显式的"记忆价值"度量。现有方法无法在不解耦黑盒描述、检索与推理流程内部机制的情况下，评估候选记忆对下游问答的价值，因此需要一种可测量并优化记忆质量的新范式。

Method: 提出生成对抗考试（GAE），利用前向仿真评估记忆检索、用考试分数量化记忆质量，从而在不访问黑盒流水线内部机制的情况下度量候选记忆的问答价值。在此基础上构建记忆中心（MemCen）框架，在通信约束下联合选择无人机并分配发射功率以最大化记忆质量。在噪声受限区推导出感知记忆质量（QoM）的封顶注水定律，将任务效用与物理层功率分配显式关联，并进一步提出惩罚连续优化（PSO）与"学会记忆"（L2M）求解器。

Result: MemCen在CARLA Town04与Town05中，静态与动态通信条件下分别达到92.4%和84.0%的问答精度；在真实世界的全景多智能体系统（PMAS）基准上达到88.5%的问答精度。无人机到机器狗的演示进一步验证了所获记忆在环境理解与导航中的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将记忆价值作为显式优化目标引入低空问答的资源分配问题，所提出的GAE度量与MemCen框架在仿真与真实场景中均有效，且封顶注水定律为任务效用与功率分配建立了可解析的联系，验证了记忆驱动的无人机协同对下游问答与导航任务的实际效用。

Abstract: This paper studies low-altitude question answering (LAQA), in which distributed unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) memories are aggregated at a ground server to answer questions about observations over a long horizon. Unlike conventional resource allocation based on sensing, communication, control, or computation metrics, LAQA requires an explicit measure of memory value. We propose a generative adversarial exam (GAE) that uses forward simulation to evaluate memory retrieval and exam scores to quantify memory quality. This enables the downstream QA value of candidate memories to be measured and optimized without accessing the internal mechanisms of the black-box captioning, retrieval, and reasoning pipeline. Building on this metric, we develop a memory-centric (MemCen) framework that jointly selects UAVs and allocates transmit power to maximize memory quality under communication constraints. In the noise-limited regime, we derive a QoM-aware capped water-filling law that explicitly connects task utility with physical-layer power allocation. We further develop penalty successive optimization (PSO) and learning to memorize (L2M) solvers. MemCen achieves QA accuracies of 92.4% and 84.0% in CARLA Town04 and Town05 under static and dynamic communication conditions, respectively. In real-world experiments, MemCen achieves 88.5% QA accuracy on the panoramic multi-agent system (PMAS) benchmark. Finally, UAV-to-robot-dog demonstrations further validate the practical utility of the acquired memories for environmental understanding and navigation.

</details>


### [173] [Self-Evolving Coding Agents: From Digital Programs to Physical-World Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35432)
*Hongcheng Gao,Jingjing Zhou,Zelin Zheng,Shijia Ge,Jay Zhu,Yazhe Wang,Jianshu Zeng,Xuan Shangguan,Di Wu,Lingyu He,Zhiqi Jia,Sihang Wu,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"Physical Coding"范式，将物理任务状态与执行表示为代码，构建可调用感知/规划/控制工具的智能体 HexaAnything，在 RoboCasa365、PhyBench 及双臂机器人上验证其泛化性与长程执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 与 WAM 模型将观测与指令直接映射为动作，导致策略与训练数据强绑定：布局或视角的微小变化即引发失败，指令泛化能力差。根本原因在于表示方式——任务需求、条件、进度与失败恢复被隐式编码进动作序列，难以检查与修改。作者借鉴数字编码智能体（LLM 调用工具、验证结果、依据反馈修正可执行代码）的工作模式，认为显式状态、可控执行与可修订流程同样适用于物理世界，从而使物理经验回流为可复用的程序、记忆或证据。

Method: 提出 Physical Coding：以代码表示任务状态与执行。其中 Code as World 记录对象、关系、约束与进度；Code as Policy 组织规划、验证、恢复与执行。据此构建智能体 HexaAnything，调用感知、规划与控制工具（含 VLA/WAM 策略），并基于外部反馈进行闭环决策。经校验的执行轨迹转化为数据与记忆，支持从工具与 Harness 向模型权重、架构乃至硬件与任务设计的演化。

Result: 在 RoboCasa365 上，HexaAnything 在 Composite-Unseen 与整体成功率上优于 XR-1 VLA；其经 Harness 训练的 HexaModel 在每个数据划分上均超越基座模型，表明代码轨迹可内化物理执行能力。在 PhyBench 与双臂 AgileX 机器人上，该智能体可自主完成物理实验与多数桌面任务，部分场景快于已发表结果。

Conclusion: 研究观察到数据、模型与工具的自我演化现象。未来工作聚焦于权重内化、架构/语言/表示/任务的自主重设计，以及在制造与科学领域的部署。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action (WAM) models map observations and instructions directly to robot actions. This directness ties a policy to training: minor layout or viewpoint changes cause failure, and instructions generalize poorly. The root cause lies in representation: task requirements, conditions, progress, and failure recovery are implicitly encoded in action sequences, making them difficult to inspect or revise. Digital coding agents offer a precedent: LLMs call tools, verify results, and revise from feedback as executable code. The same working pattern of explicit state, manageable execution, and revisable procedures underlies generalization and long-horizon execution in the physical world, letting physical experience return as reusable programs, memory, or evidence. We propose Physical Coding, representing task state and execution as code. Code as World records objects, relations, constraints, and progress; Code as Policy organizes planning, verification, recovery, and execution. We build HexaAnything, which calls perception, planning, and control tools, including VLA/WAM policies, and makes in-the-loop decisions from external feedback. Verified traces become data and memory, enabling evolution from tools and Harness to model weights, architectures, and ultimately hardware and task design. On RoboCasa365, HexaAnything improves Composite-Unseen and overall success over XR-1 VLA, and its Harness-trained HexaModel beats the base on every split, indicating code traces internalize physical execution. On PhyBench and a dual-arm AgileX robot, the agent autonomously completes physics experiments and most tabletop tasks, often faster than published results. We observe data, model, and tool self-evolution; future work targets weight internalization, autonomous redesign of architectures, languages, representations, and tasks, and deployment in manufacturing and science.

</details>


### [174] [Revision, Not Restart: Revisable Visual Plans for Closed-Loop World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35439)
*Pengyiang Liu,Junbo Niu,Wenhao Zheng,Xinchen Chen,Canyu Li,Zhongyue Shi,Jiahao Xie,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可修订时序规划（RTP），将预测的视觉未来作为持续动作条件，在反馈后通过"修订桥"恢复视觉生成中间状态并适配当前观测，实现反馈驱动的视觉规划修订与闭环任务性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型用预测的视觉未来条件化机器人动作，但执行反馈可能使部分预测失效，而任务结构仍具价值。因此需要一种能保留有用结构、同时根据反馈修订未来规划的机制。

Method: RTP将视觉未来维护为持续的动作条件，并在反馈后修订。核心是学习到的修订桥：恢复视觉生成过程中保存的中间状态，并使其后续生成适配当前观测；视觉与动作监督将该修订与后续控制相连接。时间感知历史提供观测证据，自适应策略在新噪声基础上从保留、桥接修订或全新重规划三者中选择，然后解码下一动作。

Result: 在RoboMME和RMBench上，RTP分别取得48.6%和84.8%的任务平均成功率。匹配对比支持学习到的续接；对检查点来源与动作前缀效应的估计为正向，但精度较低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，反馈驱动的视觉规划修订可有效连接闭环任务性能，说明在视觉未来部分失效时进行结构化修订是可行且有益的方向。

Abstract: World-action models use predicted visual futures to condition robot actions, yet execution feedback can invalidate parts of a prediction while leaving its task structure useful. We propose Revisable Temporal Planning (RTP), which maintains the visual future as a persistent action condition and revises it after feedback. Its central mechanism is a learned revision bridge: it resumes an intermediate state saved during visual generation and adapts its continuation to current observations. Visual and action supervision connect this revision to subsequent control. Time-aware history supplies observed evidence, and an adaptive policy selects retention, bridge revision, or fresh replanning from new noise before decoding the next action. On RoboMME and RMBench, RTP achieves task-averaged success rates of 48.6% and 84.8%, respectively. Matched comparisons support learned continuation; estimated checkpoint-source and action-prefix effects are positive but less precisely resolved. These results connect feedback-driven visual-plan revision to closed-loop task performance. Project Page: https://PLACEHOLDER.github.io/RTP/

</details>


### [175] [Uni-VLaT: Whole-Body Tactile Adaptation of VLA Policies for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35450)
*Zihao Wang,Shutong Liu,Siqi Zheng,Liu Cao,Ruoqi Chen,Rundong Liu,Yanchao Yang,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Uni-VLaT，将全身分布式触觉感知以"预测式"方式融入 VLA 策略，在五类真实机器人任务上平均成功率 75%，显著优于无触觉与无预测监督的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在移动操作中常需依据物理接触决定响应，但视觉与本体感知难以刻画物理交互，尤其在接触区域被遮挡时；相比在预设位置的稀疏力/力矩测量，分布式触觉能保留全身空间分辨的接触模式，因此需要研究如何将全身触觉信息整合进 VLA 策略以支持富接触控制。

Method: Uni-VLaT 引入一条触觉通路，其隐状态不仅用于动作生成，还被训练来预测未来的触觉、本体感知与视觉表征；该预测目标构建了以触觉为锚点的多模态上下文，促使模型形成更结构化的物理世界理解。

Result: 在五类真实机器人任务（触觉触发移动、持续物理交互、人机接触、移动操作）上平均成功率 75%，比无触觉基线高 43 个百分点，比有触觉但无预测监督的基线高 7 个百分点；在两个预训练 VLA 主干上，Table Sweeping 提升 30 点，Back-Tap Walking 提升 85–90 点；消融显示上下文触觉预测与绝对未来目标是性能关键。

Conclusion: 预测式触觉学习为将预训练 VLA 策略扩展到全身物理交互提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Physical contact often determines how a humanoid should respond during loco-manipulation, yet vision and proprioception alone are often insufficient to characterize physical interaction, especially when the contact region is occluded. Unlike sparse force or torque measurements at predefined regions, distributed tactile sensing preserves spatially resolved contact patterns across the robot body. We therefore study how to integrate such whole-body tactile information into vision-language-action (VLA) policies for contact-rich control. Our approach, Uni-VLaT, introduces a tactile pathway whose latent state is trained not only for action generation, but also to predict future tactile, proprioceptive, and visual representations. This predictive objective builds a tactile-anchored multimodal context, encouraging a more structured understanding of the physical world. We evaluate Uni-VLaT on five real-robot tasks covering tactile-triggered locomotion, sustained physical interaction, human-robot contact, and loco-manipulation. Uni-VLaT achieves a 75% average success rate, outperforming a baseline without tactile input by 43 points and a tactile-input baseline without predictive supervision by 7 points. Across two pretrained VLA backbones, our method improves Table Sweeping by 30 points on both backbones and Back-Tap Walking by 85-90 points. Ablations further show that contextualized tactile prediction and absolute future targets are critical to performance. These results indicate that predictive tactile learning provides an effective route for extending pretrained VLA policies to whole-body physical interaction.

</details>


### [176] [Rethinking Causal Action Tokenization with Conditional Annealing in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35469)
*Chenyu Zhang,Yuhang Cao,Daru Du,Yingxi Lu,Jing Shao,Ruoqu Chen,Jiajun Liu,Liu Cao,Yicheng Liu,Hang Zhao,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CATok因果动作分词器，将分词重构为因果结构化生成过程，实现与自回归VLA主干语义对齐的离散化动作表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归VLA模型的动作分词器把分词视为压缩问题，产生的表征与自回归主干语义错位，且难以清洁分离高层语义推理与底层运动执行。

Method: CATok引入条件退火机制，通过逐步退火的流匹配过程提取动作token，每个token以所有前序token为条件并编码特定噪声水平下的残差重建信号，形成由粗到细的因果token空间；再以基于MMDiT的token条件流匹配解码器从离散token重建连续动作块，无需显式注意力掩码即可实现知识隔离。

Result: 在三个仿真基准与真实机器人操作任务上，CATok在重建保真度-压缩权衡和推理效率上均超越现有分词方法，同时提升VLA任务成功率与训练效率。

Conclusion: CATok为纯自回归VLA系统建立了高性能、可扩展的基础，证明因果结构化离散分词是有效的动作表征方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a scalable path to robot learning, yet existing action tokenizers treat tokenization as a compression problem, producing representations that are semantically misaligned with the autoregressive backbone. We propose CATok, a causal action tokenizer that reframes tokenization as a causally structured generative process. CATok introduces a conditional annealing mechanism that extracts action tokens by progressively annealing a flow-matching process: each token is conditioned on all preceding tokens and encodes the residual reconstruction signal at a specific noise level, establishing a coarse-to-fine causal token space whose generative semantics are structurally aligned with autoregressive modeling. A token-conditioned flow-matching decoder built on Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (MMDiT) reconstructs continuous action chunks from these discrete tokens with the precision of hybrid diffusion-head architectures. This discrete bottleneck enforces knowledge insulation by design, cleanly separating high-level semantic reasoning from low-level motor execution without requiring explicit attention masking. Extensive evaluations across three simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that CATok consistently surpasses existing tokenization methods in both reconstruction fidelity-compression tradeoff and inference efficiency, while improving VLA task success rate and training efficiency, establishing a high-performance, scalable foundation for purely autoregressive VLA systems.

</details>


### [177] [CoBrush: A Hierarchical Planning Framework for Human-Robot Co-Painting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35476)
*Dantong Qin,Yike Guo,Qinlin Liu,Alessandro Bozzon,Pan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoBrush分层框架，将多轮人机共画建模为语义、空间、执行三层协调过程，在真实画布上实现渐进式协作绘画，效果优于单轮基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考驱动绘画或反应式助手主要面向单次渲染或草图补全，难以在人类意图随交互演变的条件下维持连贯的多轮协作，也无法在长时间内构建内容丰富的复杂场景。

Method: CoBrush采用分层框架，将多轮共同绘画分解为高层意图推理、空间落地与笔画级控制三个协调阶段，从而在真实丙烯画布上支持场景的渐进式发展。

Result: 通过真实人机绘画会话、压力测试与用户研究评估，相比单轮基线在语义对齐、空间推进稳定性以及机器人动作感知合理性上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 结构化多阶段推理可提升交互式绘画的连贯性与鲁棒性，并支撑内容丰富的实体艺术作品的渐进式创作。

Abstract: Embodied co-painting requires a robot to repeatedly update a shared physical canvas while human intent evolves over interaction. Existing reference-driven painters or reactive assistants are typically optimized for single-shot rendering or sketch completion, limiting their ability to sustain coherent multi-round collaboration or to construct complex, content-rich scenes over time. We present CoBrush, a hierarchical framework that formulates multi-round co-painting as a coordinated semantic, spatial, and execution process. By separating high-level intent inference from spatial grounding and stroke-level control, the system supports progressive scene development on real acrylic canvases. We evaluate the framework through real human-robot painting sessions, stress tests, and user studies. Compared to single-turn baselines, our approach achieves stronger semantic alignment, more stable spatial progression, and higher perceived plausibility of robot actions. These results demonstrate that structured multi-stage reasoning improves the coherence and robustness of interactive painting and supports the progressive development of content-rich physical artworks.

</details>


### [178] [Robot Tool Design from Scratch via Behavior-Aware Hierarchical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35479)
*Yinghan Chen,Xiyao Tian,Yizan Dai,Yuyang Li,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HOT分层优化框架，从零开始联合设计工具的结构、形状与动作，通过上层离散结构搜索（BASS）与下层物理优化协同，在四项工具使用任务中以极少评估量发现功能性结构，并成功迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具设计能力是超越单纯理解、选择或使用工具的更高级智能体现。现有机器人工具设计方法通常只优化工具的连续形状与动作，而工具结构由预设或预先生成的方案决定，导致结构本身脱离物理优化循环，无法实现从任务物理结果出发的端到端设计。

Method: 提出HOT（Hierarchical Optimization for Tools）分层优化方法：上层使用BASS在离散工具结构空间中进行搜索，下层进行物理优化以评估任务行为，并将"里程碑式进展"作为行为证据反馈给上层搜索，最终联合输出优化后的形状与动作，使结构、形状、动作三者共同源自期望的物理结果。

Result: 在四项具有不同物理功能的工具使用任务上，HOT仅需评估搜索空间中极小一部分（搜索空间最多含5600万个结构）即可发现功能性结构；随后对几何形状的细化在所有任务上降低了任务损失并保持成功率，且变形在功能上可解释；3D打印后的工具能在真实机器人上以仿真中找到的动作完成全部任务。

Conclusion: 从所需物理效应而非已知工具目录出发设计工具，是迈向人类与动物那般开放式工具制造的重要一步。

Abstract: The ability to design a tool for a task marks a level of intelligence beyond merely understanding, selecting, or using one. Existing methods for robotic tool design typically optimize a tool's continuous shape and action within a structure that is prescribed or generated beforehand, so the structure itself stays outside the physical optimization loop. We study task-driven tool design from scratch, where tool structure, shape, and action are all derived from the desired physical outcome. Here we show that the three elements can be designed jointly by HOT, a hierarchical optimization whose upper level searches over discrete tool structures with BASS, while lower-level physical optimization evaluates their task behavior and returns milestone progress as behavioral evidence for the search, ultimately providing jointly optimized shape and action. On four tool-use tasks with distinct physical functions, HOT discovers functional structures after evaluating only a small fraction of search spaces containing up to 56 million structures, and the subsequent refinement of their geometry lowers the task loss on all tasks while preserving success, through deformations that are functionally interpretable. Once 3D printed, the tools accomplish all tasks on a real robot with the actions found in simulation. Designing tools from required physical effects, rather than a catalog of known tools, is a step toward the open-ended tool making seen in humans and animals.

</details>


### [179] [ForVis: An In-Field Dataset and Benchmark for VIO Using Under-Canopy UAV Flights in Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35482)
*Arman Kiani,Masoud Ataei,Elvis Gyaase,Jeffrey Eiyike,Aaron Weiskittel,Prabuddha Chakraborty,Vikas Dhiman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ForVis，一个用于在林间无人机飞行中评估VI-SLAM的实飞数据集与基准。数据包含三种林间场景下12次飞行、563.8秒、1096.8米轨迹，并同步采集双相机与惯性/飞控数据。对7个开源VI-SLAM系统进行504次测试，发现传感器选择对轨迹误差的影响大于算法差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机VI-SLAM在真实森林环境中难以评估，因为运动、光照变化、重复植被和振动都会影响估计效果。现有评估难以覆盖这些挑战性条件，因此需要一个真实的林间飞行数据集与基准。
